nardamānaḥ mahā svanaḥ devyāḥ panthānam āvarya dānava indra sutaḥ sthitaḥ vijñāya tasya āgamanam yoga īśāḥ vindhyavāsinī atha dṛṣṭvā sa tām devīm krodhāt raktatara īkṣaṇaḥ abhyājagāma vegena yatnam āsthāya dānavaḥ udbāladhiḥ tīkṣṇa viṣāṇa koṭiḥ su pīna vakṣāḥ kaṭhina ūrdhva karṇaḥ tam kauśikī vīkṣya tadā āpatantam daitya indra putram mahiṣam sa roṣam abhyetya tām dānava rāja sūnuḥ balena mattaḥ parameṇa devīm abhyāhanat vakṣasi lola hāre śṛṅgeṇa śakrāyudha karkaśena viṣahya tam tasya tadā prahāram jagrāha śṛṅge mahiṣam kareṇa udgṛhya sā bāladhim indra śatroḥ kṛtvā ca pādam śirasi prasahya triśūlam ādāya bibheda pṛṣṭhe vyayojayat ca asubhiḥ āśu daityam kuliśa kaṭhina śṛṅgam nīla jīmūta kalpam mahiṣam atha nihatya prauḍha darpam tadānīm vividha kusuma vṛṣṭyā sarvatas kīryamāṇā dharaṇidharam agacchat kauśikī sannivāsam vyāsaḥ uvāca tapaḥ kṛtvā tadā devī puṣkalam himavat sutā somanandī ca śārdūlaḥ kim avāpa varam śubham yam paṭhanti sadā sāṃkhyāḥ puruṣam paṃcaviṃsakam praṇamya parayā bhaktyā śirasā tam trilocanam gauryā samāgamaḥ vyāsa śaṅkarasya yathā bhavat tat pravakṣyāmi te sarvam śṛṇu yoga vidām vara tasmin himavataḥ śṛṅge tapaḥ kṛtvā su duṣkaram gaura aṅgī sā tadā bhūtvā śuddha hema sama prabhā darśane śaṅkarasya atha śarvāṇī matim ādadhe nandinam gaṇapam devī dīpta paṭṭiśa dhāriṇam tatas nandīśvaram dṛṣṭvā bhaktam premṇā girīndrajā ehi vatsa cirāt dṛṣṭaḥ tvam iti uktavatī tadā nandī saṃprekṣya tām ca api gaura aṅgīm hema saprabhām praṇamya sa tadā dhīmān astuvat parvatātmajām tvam kriyā kāraṇam tvam ca prakṛtiḥ muktiḥ eva ca āntiḥ dyutiḥ tathā tuṣṭiḥ śāntiḥ svastyayanam param dakṣiṇā niyatiḥ mṛtyuḥ sandhyā vidyut ahar kṣapā pṛthivī kauśikī śrīḥ ca śarvāṇī śarva vallabhā gaṇamātā ditiḥ ca eva pavitrā vindhyavāsinī atha devī tadā dṛṣṭvā nandinam pādayoḥ natam evam astu iti sā procya praviveśa āyata īkṣaṇā agratas yayau nandī somanandī ca pṛṣṭhatas ājagāma mahā tejāḥ saṃbhrama utphulla locanaḥ atha devaḥ tadā āyātām devīm giri vara ātmajām samuvāca suraśreṣṭhaḥ samālokya muhur muhur diṣṭyā asi prīta vadanā diṣṭyā ca saphalam tapaḥ priyam naḥ sarvathā devi paramam yat tvam āgatā pratijñām pūrayitvā iha śārdūlaḥ a ayam kutas ca te evam uktā tadā devī bhartrā praṇaya peśalam kathayāmāsa devāya sarvam caritam ātmanaḥ prāk aham yat tvayā deva kṛṣṇa varṇā iti bhāṣitā tadā manyu parīta aṅgī gaura varṇa samīpsayā tasmin eva eṣa śārdūlaḥ somanandī mama agratas tāvantam kālam eṣaḥ mama atiṣṭhat samīpatas stabdha lāṅgūla netra āsyaḥ nirāhāraḥ vilokayan tam aham pūrvam asya arthe varam saṃprārthayam vibho mama eva anucaraḥ pārśve kṛtaḥ yogī ca śaṅkara varṇam ca idam adāt mahyam tava ādeśāt pitāmahaḥ mṛgendraḥ gaṇa śreṣṭhaḥ soma nandī mahā balaḥ yādṛśaḥ mama nandīśaḥ tādṛśaḥ te bhaviṣyati tam bhūyas a api tvayā sārdham draṣṭum icchāmi śaṅkara nandinam sa tadā āhūya gaṇapān ca sahasraśas nandinaḥ pṛṣṭham ārūḍhaḥ prakrīḍita gaṇa īśvaraḥ sa tam śikharam āsādya ramya dhātu vibhūṣitam āgatam tam atho dṛṣṭvā mūrimān acalaḥ tadā vara dānena śaila indram anvagṛhṇāt pinākadhṛk puṇyaḥ tvam acala śreṣṭha bhavitā pāpa mocanaḥ dūrāt eva naga śreṣṭha dṛṣṭvā tvām ucchritam janāḥ dṛṣṭvā gaurīm atha abhyarcya tarpayiṣyati nārī vā atha naraḥ vā api lokam gauryāḥ sa yāsyati yaḥ ca iha tyakṣyate prāṇān niyamena samāhitaḥ sa gāṇapatyam samprāpya mayā sārdham cariṣyati gauryāḥ ca atrā eva sānnidhyam sarvadā te bhaviṣyati athā apaśyat śilām vyāsa sauvarṇām maṇi bhūṣitām kathayāmāsa tām devyai śilām paśya iti a prabhuḥ tam āha devī deveśam iha taptam mayā tapaḥ atha tām anujagrāha śilām prītyā bhavaḥ tadā śuciḥ asyām nirāhāram tri rātram yaḥ kariṣyati eka pādena yaḥ martyaḥ tiṣṭhet ekam ahar śuciḥ prāṇān parityajet yaḥ ca śilāyām iha dustyajān athā apṛcchat punar devaḥ pārvatīm vicaran prabhuḥ katham etāni kuṇḍāni babhūvuḥ iha śailaje evam pṛṣṭā punar devī vākyam etat jagāda ha ete tābhyām kṛte kuṇḍe sita toye mahā plave tapaḥ taptum alaṅkārān aham tyaktavatī yadā tayā ca idam apām kuṇḍam dvitīyam me kṛtam śubham śarīrāt me tadā deva kṛṣṇā kośī viniṣkramat tasyām kośyām samutpannam etat kuṇḍam tṛtīyakam idam kṛṣṇājinam śubhram sthāpitam tat purā mayā pratyuvāca tadā devīm prītyā paramayā yutaḥ stana toyena yat pūrṇe tava kuṇḍe śaśiprabhe upavāsī nataḥ yaḥ snātvā abhyarcya ca devatāḥ alaṃkārāt srutā dhārā yā iyam tava varānane eṣā alaṃkāradhārā iti nāmnā khyātim gamiṣyati nīla toyam idam yat te mahat kuṇḍam śubha udakam agni kāryam iha snātvā yaḥ kuryāt brāhmaṇaḥ śuciḥ agnihotram hutam tena bhavet svādaśa vārṣikam gṛhītvā pādapa sthāni valkalāni tadā prabhuḥ tāni abhūvan tadā vyāsa nadī svaccha jalā śubhā taṭa jaiḥ vividhaiḥ vṛkṣaiḥ haṃsa cakra āhva śobhitā tām uvāca sura śreṣṭhaḥ puṇya toyām mahā nadīm cīrebhyaḥ nissṛtā yasmāt tvam atra saritām varā loke puṇyatamā tasmāt niścīrā iti bhaviṣyasi mahā phalam mahā puṇyam sarva siddhi pradam śubham iha upoṣya śuciḥ snātvā bhaktyā paramayā yutaḥ tat eva labhate devi dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇājinam dvijaḥ āvayoḥ gaṇapaḥ bhūtvā jagat sarvam cariṣyati evam naga indram sa tadā anugṛhya muni indra sārdham girirāja putryā kośī śilā stana kuṇḍa ajina gaurīśikhara vara pradānam nāma vyāsaḥ uvāca cāru sopāna saṃkīrṇe citra paṭṭa śilā tale sahasrākṣaḥ śaśāṅkaḥ ca vāyuḥ ca eva tadā gatāḥ samāgamya pura dvāram dṛṣṭvā te nandinam sthitam ūcuḥ prāñjalayaḥ bhūtvā śaṅkaram saṃdidṛkṣavaḥ atha nandī samāgatya dvāra mūle samāsthitaḥ śambhoḥ nivedayāmāsa trīn devān tu samāgatān viviśuḥ te trayaḥ devāḥ śaṅkarasya antikam tadā atha tān prāha lokeśaḥ śirobhiḥ praṇatān sthitān brūta dāsyāmi tat sarvam yat yat mṛgayatha amarāḥ atha devaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ vacaḥ provāca śaṅkaram yat cakāra vapuḥ viṣṇuḥ nārasiṃham bhayānakam tam hatvā api sa daitya indram viṣṇuḥ para bala ardanaḥ tena rūpeṇa deveśa krūreṇa api hita īpsunā na vayam nirvṛtāḥ bhūtvā trāsāt tiṣṭhāma śaṅkara sa yathā siṃha rūpam tat parityajati mādhavaḥ siṃha rūpam yathā śakra viṣṇuḥ tyakṣyati bhīṣaṇam atha vāyuḥ tadā devam vyajñāpayata śaṃkaram aśarīraḥ yathā na syām tathā me kartum arhasi śarīram su mahat prādāt yena asau mūrtimān abhūt vijñāpitam śaśāṅkena tatas vyāsa mahātmanā dakṣeṇa aham purā śaptaḥ yakṣma dehaḥ bhaviṣyasi sa yathā yakṣma hīnaḥ syām prasādam me tathā kuru vijñaptim śaśinaḥ śrutvā devaḥ tasmai vacaḥ prabhuḥ idam prāha mahā tejāḥ śaśine praṇatāya tu tapaḥ kṛtvā prabhāse tvam yakṣma hīnaḥ bhaviṣyasi vyāsaḥ uvāca kim vīryam kim tapaḥ ca asya śaṃsa etat pṛcchataḥ mama abhiṣiktaḥ sa daityānām rājā abhūt brahmaṇā purā atha teṣām tadā vyāsa trailokyam saṃpraśāsatām hiraṇyakaśipuḥ daityaḥ śakra rājya jihīrṣayā sa gatvā dakṣiṇām āśām śrīparvata samāsthitaḥ sva raktena juhāva agnau saṃmiśrāḥ samidhaḥ carūn ūrdhvabāhuḥ nirālambaḥ divā sūrya arpita īkṣaṇaḥ ekapādaḥ aanimeṣaḥ ca saḥ aatiṣṭhat kṛta niścayaḥ dve sahasre tadā abdānām divyānām sthitavān balī athā abhyetya sura śreṣṭhaḥ brahmā kamala vāhanaḥ iṣṭam vṛṇu varam rājan iti provāca sādaram varam prārthitavān vyāsa śṛṇu yādṛśam īpsitam amaraḥ syām avadhyaḥ ca jarā hīnaḥ mahā balaḥ na eva ārdreṇa na śuṣkeṇa na puṃsā na ca yoṣitā jagāma ātma puram kṣipram śāntaḥ prītaḥ pitāmahaḥ saḥ sarva asura śreṣṭhaḥ hiraṇyakaśipuḥ tadā jitvā śakram svayam rājyam cakāra diti nandanaḥ trailokyam ātmasātkṛtvā jitvā sarvān divaukasaḥ ātmanā eva abhavat śakraḥ svayam vāyuḥ svayam yamaḥ praṇamadhvam yajadhvam mām brahmā aham sa purandaraḥ iti prāha ṛṣīn sarvān siddhān viprān ca saḥ a asuraḥ vyāsaḥ uvāca katham śrīparvataḥ a asau vai kim puṇyam tatra kim phalam tapaḥ siddhān mahā yogān jvalana āditya varcasaḥ kṛtvā pradakṣiṇām śambhoḥ sa praṇāmam mahā mune ke ete deva saṃsiddhāḥ yogi īśāḥ tvām praṇemire yoga siddhāḥ mahātmānaḥ yoge pāśupate sthitāḥ ye iha ārādhya mām devi jahuḥ prāṇān nara uttamāḥ mokṣa siddheḥ parām niṣṭhām gantāraḥ paramam padam devam provāca śarvāṇī vacaḥ parama pūjitam yathā aham api deveśa prāpnuyām siddhim īdṛśīm kariṣyāmi tathā yatnam eṣā ca asmi gatā vibho tatas kṛtvā śriyaḥ rūpam devī parama śobhanā devaḥ ca asyāḥ varam prādāt taiḥ samānam mahā dyutiḥ mahā yoga īśvarī siddhā para artha jñā bhaviṣyasi ayam ca parvataḥ śrīmān nāmnā tava bhaviṣyasi śrīparvataḥ iti khyātaḥ citra rūpa vibhūṣitaḥ śuciḥ snātvā arcayet martyaḥ sa lokam mama yāsyati yaḥ tu iha tyakṣyate prāṇān śrīgirau acala uttame trinetraḥ kāmagaḥ bhūtvā gāṇapatyam avāpnuyāt iha āgatya ca ye śailam kariṣyanti pradakṣiṇam śarvāyatana saṃyuktam bhaviṣyanti ca te gaṇāḥ śrīgiri vara pradānam nāma darpeṇa mahatā āviṣṭaḥ śrī mada udvṛtta cetanaḥ sa devān sahitān sarvān śakra ādyān krodha mūrchitaḥ cālitān api rājye sve kṛcchram āpādayat bhṛśam trailokyam kṛtsnam ākramya rājyam naḥ hṛtavān divaḥ aniśam ca avamānan naḥ prayuṅkte vaira yātanam prasādam kuru deveśa sa na syāt naḥ yathā ripuḥ provāca idam mahā prajña devānām prita kṛt vacaḥ kṣīṇam tat daitya rājasya tapaḥ tasya durātmanaḥ kṛcchram gamitavān yaḥ vaḥ rājyam ca hṛtavān ripuḥ tena aham prārthitaḥ pūrvam sarva avadhya tvam uttamam antaram bhāṣitaḥ ca asau mayā saṃjñā vimohitaḥ tadā eva manasā toṣam aham āgām mahā balāḥ tam brūta sahitāḥ sarve tasya nāśa artham acyutam narasiṃha vapuḥ kṛtvā sakṛt āhatya haṃsyati hiraṇyakaśipum daityam tatas nivṛtim eṣyatha ājagmuḥ sahitāḥ devāḥ viṣṇoḥ bhavanam ādṛtāḥ atha dṛṣṭvā mahā tejāḥ viṣṇuḥ devān upasthitān pūjyāḥ bhavantaḥ sarve me suhṛdaḥ yat vaḥ manaḥ gatam kiṃcid mayā kāryam divaukasaḥ kṣipram tat brūta saṃkṣiptam kartāham avicāritam ūcuḥ prahṛṣṭa vadanāḥ saṃstuvantaḥ janārdanam bhayam naḥ su mahat jātam rājyam ca apahṛtam divaḥ tatas viṣṇuḥ ciram dhyātvā vadha upāyam pracintya ca prāha devān suraśreṣṭhaḥ vacasā āpyāyayan iva āviśantu bhavantaḥ śarīram mama suvratāḥ sarva deva mayaḥ bhūtvā dṛptam haṃsyāmi vaḥ ripum tatas devāḥ tadā sarve viviśuḥ vaiṣṇavīm tanum sa ca api balavān bhūtvā rūpam kṛtvā bhayānakam nārasiṃham mahā tejāḥ nakha daṃṣṭrā vibhūṣaṇam jagāma vilasat viṣṇuḥ hiraṇyakaśipoḥ puram nṛsiṃhaḥ daitya siṃhasya dṛsṭvā tasthau su vismitaḥ vṛkṣaiḥ kāma phalaiḥ ca eva vaibhrāja sadṛśam vanam tat babhañja nṛsiṃhaḥ tu khela gāmī mahā utkaṭaḥ sarāṃsi kṣobhayāmāsa karī iva sa mada ulvaṇaḥ athā abhyetya nṛsiṃham tam vanapālāḥ ruṣā anvitāḥ balinaḥ vārayāmāsuḥ tān ca eva abhyahanat balī hata śeṣāḥ tatas tatra pravilīnāḥ tu ye a asurāḥ te gatvā kathayāmāsuḥ hiraṇyakaśipoḥ tadā prahlādam ca anuhlādam ca namucim bāṣkalim balim provāca tvaritam gatvā baddhvā siṃham mama antikam krīḍaṇam siṃha potaḥ devyāḥ mama bhaviṣyati udyānam sahitāḥ gatvā nṛsiṃham dadṛśuḥ sthitam dūra sthāḥ cikṣipuḥ pāśān tān sa ciccheda mādhavaḥ atha vidrāvayāmāsa dānavān keśarī bhṛśam pārśvam dānava rājasya sarvam ca akathayan tadā atha kruddhaḥ svayam rājā hiraṇyakaśipuḥ balī ratham āsthāya sannaddhā nṛsiṃham prādravat ruṣā sa rathaḥ siṃha rājena sa āyudhaḥ parvata upamaḥ sambhrāmya pātitaḥ tūrṇam cūrṇam āgāt mahī tale vṛkṣam utpāṭya vegena nṛsiṃhāya vyasarjayat atha asau patitaḥ gātre siṃha rājasya pādapaḥ mṛd khaṇḍaḥ iva pāṣāṇe kṣiptaḥ cūrṇam jagāma ha gṛhītvā sa tadā siṃhaḥ hiraṇyakaśipum sakṛt divaḥ rājyam punar śakraḥ lebhe niṣkaṇṭakam mahat viṣṇoḥ tyājayitum rūpam saiṃham adbhuta karmaṇaḥ śarabhaḥ sa tadā bhūtvā himavat śikhara upamaḥ caturbhiḥ pṛṣṭha jaiḥ pādaiḥ tīkṣṇa daṃṣṭraḥ mahā balaḥ narasiṃha samīpam tu gatvā agarjat samāhitaḥ atha siṃhaḥ tadā dṛṣṭvā śarabham samupasthitam ātmanā eva agamat kṛcchram sparśāt tasya mahātmanaḥ tatas viṣṇuḥ ciram dhyātvā jñātvā śaṃkaram āgatam kālāya yama rūpāya kāla daṇḍāya vai namaḥ namaḥ kāla anta kartre ca kālekāla harāya ca caṇḍāya vāmadevāya sarva yoga īśvarāya ca śarabhāya su rūpāya vyāghra carma su vāsase indriyāṇām atha īśāya manasaspataye namaḥ śaraṇam tvām prapannaḥ a aham prasīda mama sarvadā śrāvaṇam śravaṇam ca asya tathā anusmaraṇam sadā hṛṣīkeśam uvāca idam vacaḥ parama pūjitam tubhyam viṣṇo mayā dattaḥ puṇyaḥ hi eṣa varaḥ śubhaḥ sa tvam viṣṇuḥ mahā tejāḥ mattaḥ labdha varaḥ sadā velāyām tvām samudraḥ tu tiryagyonim asūta yaḥ ehi gaccha śubhām yonim ātmanaḥ parama adbhutām tatas tam ākramat pādaiḥ siṃham śarabha sattamaḥ viṣṇave a atha varam dattvā daitya ghnam sa vṛṣadhvajaḥ śarabha kṛta narasiṃha uddhāra ākhyānam nāma vyāsaḥ uvāca apanīya tadā viṣṇoḥ siṃha rūpam vṛṣadhvajaḥ sva yonim viṣṇum ānīya devadevaḥ bhavaḥ tadā vana prekṣā ratam devam jñātvā śaṃkaram avyayam tatas mahīruhāḥ sarve sarāṃsi ca samantataḥ nānāvidhāni puṣpāṇi mumucuḥ sahasā samam kamala utpala saṃchannāḥ ṣaṭpada vrāta sevitāḥ haṃsa sārasa saṃvāsāḥ vāpyaḥ tatra manoharāḥ gandha āḍhyāḥ jātayaḥ kṣipram babhūvuḥ saha campakaiḥ aśokāḥ gandhapuṣpāḥ ca tathā ca eva atimuktakāḥ sarva ṛtu kusuma ākīrṇam dṛṣṭvā tat vanam uttamam puṣpāṇi ādāya deveśaḥ svayam eva jagatpatiḥ alaṃcakāra hṛṣṭa ātmā devīm himagireḥ sutām nandīśvaram ca pārśva stham ātmānam ca sureśvaraḥ provāca idam mahā tejāḥ devyāḥ priya cikīrṣayā evam bhavatu deveśe vijñaptim saphalām imām śreṣṭhaḥ sarva sura īśānām mahā yoga bala anvitaḥ prārthitaḥ hi eṣa putra artham tena avaśyam dadāmi te nandīśena tadā sārdham vicarantau jagadgurū darī kandara saṃkīrṇam nānā nirjhara saṃyutam sa taḍit megha saṃghāta ghanīkṛta śilā uccayam nānā vṛkṣa śilā upetam nānā kusuma saṃyutam mānasam tasya madhye tu puram sṛṣṭvā mahā ṛddhimat nandinam ca tatas devī samājñāpayat avyayā vatsa nandin tathā dvāre tiṣṭheḥ tvam gaṇapa īśvara viśet na iha yathā kaścid sūkṣmaḥ vāyuḥ api sthitaḥ kṛtvā śirasi tām ājñām nandīśaḥ gaṇapa adhipaḥ tasthatuḥ su ciram kālam tanaya artham mahā dyutī brahmaṇaḥ madvidhānām ca śreṣṭhaḥ a asau parameśvaraḥ atas vakṣyāmi te vyāsa priyaḥ śiṣyaḥ ca me bhavān atītam sthitayoḥ divyam sahasram śaradām abhūt nadyaḥ śoṣam tadā jagmuḥ cukṣubhe saritām patiḥ tapanaḥ niṣprabhaḥ ca āsīt vavāv uṣṇaḥ samīraṇaḥ jagāma śaraṇam viṣṇum gatvā ca ārtim nyavedayat utpātāḥ dāruṇāḥ deva babhūvuḥ bahavaḥ bhṛśam tena atīva samudvignā tvām adya śaraṇam gatā atha viṣṇuḥ surān sarvān idam aśrāvayat prabhuḥ ekatra saṃgatāḥ bhūtvā cakruḥ bahuvidhāḥ kathāḥ mahā buddhiḥ atha āha idam vākyam aṅgirasaḥ sutaḥ śrīkaṇṭhaḥ bhagavān eṣa devadevaḥ trilocanaḥ aga ādi uktasya pārvatyāḥ babhūvuḥ dāruṇāḥ tatas utpātāḥ jagataḥ trāsāḥ cintyate kim iha aparam gacchāma sahitāḥ sarve stotum bhaktyā vṛṣadhvajam yathā ca deha jaḥ putraḥ bhavet devyāḥ sura īśvarāḥ vijñāpayāmāsa deveśam tathā śīghram iha amarāḥ utpatsyati na ca drogdhā tatas kṣaṃsyāma tam surāḥ tatas enam aham baddhvā kāla pāśena bālakam āneṣye sva vaśam krūram mṛtyunā sahitaḥ dvidhā yena kṛtaḥ mūḍhaḥ prāṇān tyakṣyati dustyajān viṣṇuḥ provāca dharma ātmā vacāṃsi parama artha vid tanayaḥ kila yaḥ śambhoḥ girijāyām bhaviṣyati sa mūḍhaḥ iva yaḥ kaścid bhavitā vaśagaḥ tava kālena aparipūrṇena mānavasya api bhānuja na tvam nāśayitā nāma kimu putrasya śūlinaḥ sa katham vadhya tām yāyāt tava śakrasya vā vibho prayāma sahitāḥ sarve tam prasādayitum haram yaḥ naḥ svayaṃvare dattaḥ varaḥ tiṣṭhati śaṃkare na asmākam ahitam śambhuḥ putram utpādayiṣyati vayam eva sutāḥ sarve śaṃkarasya agrajāḥ vibhoḥ na śakyaḥ tapasā draṣṭum na jñānena sura īśvarāḥ bhaktyā paramayā draṣṭum śakyaḥ syāt sa vṛṣadhvajaḥ parām bhaktim samāsthāya yathā paśyeya śaṃkaram sarveṣām eva vijñaptyā surāṇām sura sattamāḥ bṛhaspati purogāḥ te hṛṣṭa ātmānaḥ divaukasaḥ sādhu sādhu iti santuṣṭāḥ vacāṃsi ūcuḥ sura uttamāḥ tatas viṣṇu sahāyāḥ te gatvā tad śṛṅgam uttamam ūcuḥ hutāśanam devam vacaḥ stuti puraḥsaram praviśya trāhi naḥ sarvān śaṃkarāya nivedaya tathā kuryāḥ suraśreṣṭha yena nirgamya śaṃkaraḥ asmān nirīkṣate devaḥ prahvān bhaktimataḥ sthitān iti uktaḥ sa suraiḥ sarvaiḥ pratyuvāca hutāśanaḥ pārvatyā saha saṃyuktam devadeveśvaram param na me śaktiḥ haram draṣṭum bhavantaḥ ca ākulāḥ bhṛśam pravekṣye dūta vat devāḥ kariṣye sāhasam mahat iti uktvā sa jagāma āśu bhavanam tat kapardinaḥ dvāram ākramya tiṣṭhantam dīpta paṭṭiśa dhāriṇam tam dṛṣṭvā sa tadā vahniḥ samālokya samantataḥ na asti kaścid iha upāyaḥ sūkṣma jantoḥ api sthite praveṣṭum iti saṃcintya tam eva śaraṇam yayau nandinam hutabhuj devaḥ tuṣṭāva ca kṛtāñjaliḥ namaḥ senā adhipataye namaḥ śarva ātmane sadā namaḥ śmaśānarudrāya koṭīrudrāya te namaḥ vara daḥ bhava nandīśa mā naḥ krodham kṛthāḥ prabho brūhi kim te priyam deva karavāṇi hutāśana deva kāryeṇa deveśa praveṣṭum dīyatām mama na tam śakṣyasi deveśam draṣṭum tvam yogam āsthitam saha devyāḥ maheśānam sraṣṭāram jagataḥ aavyayam gaccha sūkṣmataraḥ bhūtvā durālakṣyaḥ hutāśana praviśya niṣkrama kṣipram avijñāta āgama kriyaḥ sa nandinā abhyanujñātaḥ jātavedāḥ mudā yutaḥ jyotiḥ sūkṣmataraḥ bhūtvā bhāskarasya aṃśunā saha vāhneya maṇi madhya stham stambham tam bhāsayan muhur saḥ aapaśyat tatra deveśam devīm ca bhuvaneśvarīm tuṣṭāva manasā devau tatas havyavāhanaḥ tathā saṃstūyamānaḥ ca prītim devyāḥ ca darśayan vīkṣya stambham haraḥ devīm vākyam etat abhāṣata saṃstauti mām tvayā sārdham dadāni asmai varam śubham devāḥ tvām draṣṭum icchanti bahiṣthāḥ bhuvana īśvara dadāmi darśanam vahne bhavataḥ priya kāmyayā praṇemuḥ mūrdhabhiḥ hṛṣṭāḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ ca mudā yutāḥ namaḥ śmaśāna rataye vyāghra carma su vāsase namaḥ kāmeṣu asaktāya kāma saktāya vai namaḥ pratyuvāca mahādevaḥ tān surān praṇatān sthitān parituṣṭaḥ aasmi bho devāḥ brūta kim pradadāni vaḥ aśubhāni nimittāni dṛśyante su mahā dyute pṛthivī duḥsthitā deva vayam ca bhṛśam ākulāḥ tena eṣa na yathā devyāḥ dehe saṃjāyate sutaḥ mahān eṣa varaḥ sarvaiḥ prārthitaḥ sura puṅgavaiḥ tathā api ca prayacchāmi yuṣmad priya cikīrṣayā mahāntam kim tu idam kālam tejaḥ sandhāritam mayā babhūvuḥ sahitāḥ tūṣṇīm surāḥ brahma purogamāḥ teṣām madhye tadā vahniḥ provāca bhuvaneśvaram juhāva agnau tatas tejaḥ tadā svīyam param bhavaḥ hutvā tasmin tatas kṛtsnam tuṣṭaḥ aabhūt parameśvaraḥ varam vṛṇu hutāśa tvam iti provāca śaṃkaraḥ śāpasya aham agamyaḥ syām paśyeyam tvām ca nityaśas dhārayeyam sukhena etat utsṛjeyam ca śaṃkara mayi devāḥ ca yajñāḥ ca pratiṣṭhām yāntu śūla dhṛk aham bhūteśvaraḥ ca syām aham paśupatiḥ ca ha aham tava tanuḥ syām ca tvad mūrtiḥ tvad upāśrayaḥ varam icchāmi deveśa dīyamānam imam tvayā kva gatvā tvam iha āyātaḥ kṣipram tribhuvana īśvara tatas provāca deveśaḥ pārvatīm ruṣita ānanām katham tvam āgatān deva bahiḥsthān tridivaukasaḥ jñātavān asi santrastān kena ākhyātam ca te vibho tatas bhagavatī kruddhā nandinam prāha pārvatī tatas sā asya bhṛśam kruddhā śravaṇa nyastam utpalam gṛhītvā vajra saṃkāśam nandīśāya vyasarjayat dvādaśa āditya saṃkāśam prajvalat tat tadā utpalam hataḥ nandīśvaraḥ iti procya utthāya ca śaṃkaraḥ aho bata mahat kaṣṭam kṛtam krodha vaśāt mayā kathaṃcid na hataḥ nandī mahā gaṇapa nāyakaḥ tat gṛhītvā tatas hastāt devasya śravaṇa utpalam nāmnā bhadrotpalam iti hemaśṛṅga samīpataḥ tatra akṣayam sadā dānam pitṛ tarpaṇam eva ca tatas devam punar devī vākyam āha gireḥ sutā kim ūcuḥ tvām surāḥ deva kīdṛśam ca arthayan varam kathayāmāsa deveśaḥ tatas devyai vṛṣadhvajaḥ santrastā pṛthivī kṛtsnā vayam ca bhṛśam ākulāḥ tena eṣa na yathā devyāḥ garbhe saṃjāyate sutaḥ bhavet ca tanayaḥ ślāghyaḥ varaḥ eṣa pradīyatām sutaḥ te bhavitā śrīmān ṣaṣ śirāḥ dvādaśa īkṣaṇaḥ svāsu tasmāt te na prāpsyanti surāḥ sutam pṛthivī ca api saṃmūḍhā mad śāpa upahata indriyā virarāma tatas devī śaptvā devān sa vāsavān kaiṭabhā nāma nāmnā ca puṇyā nā eka vara pradā puṇyam āyatanam tat hi śilām ca ramaṇāhvayām athā ājagmuḥ surāḥ sarve sārdham tatra havirbhujā vivarṇāḥ kaśmala upetāḥ sa garbhāḥ lajjayā anvitāḥ tān dvāḥsthān āgatān nandī śaṃkarāya nyavedayat praviśya samanujñātāḥ devam ūcuḥ divaukasaḥ trāhi naḥ sura loka īśa yathā syāma gata jvarāḥ yuṣmad arthe parākrāntam hirāṇyākṣa sutam varam nāmnā śaravaṇam nāma puṇyam dvādaśa yojanam yuṣmākam tatra sambhidya kukṣīn daivata puṅgavāḥ suvarṇa tvam ca yāsyanti kukṣayaḥ vaḥ sura uttamāḥ śrīmantam merum ājagmuḥ śara dhānam ca tat śubham yatra teṣām tadā bhittvā kukṣīn teṣām nirakramīt yugānta anala saṃkāśam durnirīkṣyam sura asuraiḥ meruḥ ca eva bhavat kṣipram śrīmān ati manoharaḥ sarvam hiraṇmayam cakre tejaḥ māheśvaram param teṣām ca kukṣi bhedāḥ te suvarṇa tvam param yayuḥ puṣpāṇām vṛṣṭayaḥ petuḥ sugandhīnām anekaśas namaskāraiḥ ca bhūtānām adṛśyānām abhūt svanaḥ evamādīni paśyantaḥ śṛṇvantaḥ ca sura uttamāḥ gatvā pinākine hṛṣṭāḥ sarvam āvedya suvrata preṣitāḥ sthāṇunā jagmuḥ svān lokān hṛṣita ānanāḥ jñātvā tasthau vibudha vara dā yogamātā vidhātrī tuṣṭim ca agāt hṛdi sura śiraḥ spṛṣṭa pāda aravindā vyāsaḥ uvāca andhakaḥ kasya putraḥ a asau kiṃvīryaḥ kiṃparākramaḥ śṛṇu vyāsa mahābuddhe sarvam etat yathā bhavat jyeṣṭhaḥ tatra abhavat rājā hiraṇyakaśipuḥ tadā hiraṇyākṣaḥ abhavat rājā sarva daitya namaskṛtaḥ sa śaṃkukarṇam samprāpya sindhu sāgara saṃgame tapaḥ tepe diteḥ putraḥ devam draṣṭum pinākinam vyāsaḥ uvāca śaṃkukarṇam maheśasya kṣetram sarva artha sādhakam daśasu api tatas dikṣu rakṣaṇa artham jagatpatiḥ sthāpayāmāsa gaṇapān nikumbha ādīn sahasraśas śaṃku karṇam mahā daṃṣṭram sthūla akṣam sthūla nāsikam hrasva pāda karam ca eva dārita āsyam vibhīṣaṇam samāhūya samīpam tu bhagavān idam abravīt svaiḥ tvam gaṇeśvaraiḥ sārdham na viśeyuḥ yathā surāḥ niruddhāḥ śaṃku karṇena na celuḥ padam agratas devāḥ ūcuḥ bho gaṇeśāḥ vayam devam śarvam ugram kapardinam draṣṭum kāryeṇa samprāptāḥ tat naḥ darśayata anaghāḥ ākulāḥ tam a paśyantaḥ naśyāma pramatha īśvarāḥ śaṃkukarṇaḥ uvāca na yuṣmābhiḥ surāḥ śakyaḥ devaḥ draṣṭum kathaṃcana devāḥ ūcuḥ mā lokānām vināśaḥ syāt gaṇeśvara mahā dyute śaṃkukarṇaḥ uvāca lokāḥ naśyantu yūyam vā na praveśaḥ pradīyate vinaṣṭān punar eva īśaḥ srakṣyate bhuvaneśvaraḥ sraṣṭā saḥ eva bhūtānām sa vināśayitā tathā tam vinā kaḥ surāḥ śaktaḥ lokān nāśayitum vibhum yacchāma na vayam dvāram ājñā naḥ pārameśvarī atha brahmā surān āha mayā saha punar surāḥ gacchadhvam gaṇapān yāvat pratyāpattim nayāmi vaḥ tatas te brahmaṇā sārdham viṣṇunā ca divaukasaḥ tam eva punar ājagmuḥ tiṣṭhate yatra śaṃkaraḥ śaṃku karṇaḥ gaṇādhyakṣaḥ tejasā prajvalan iva gaṇeśvara na devānām dauṣṭyam kiṃcid hi vidyate śirasā praṇatāḥ tubhyam devāḥ ca indra purogamāḥ kruddhaḥ paṭṭiśam udyamya vacaḥ provāca niṣṭhuram na me gurutaraḥ devāḥ praṇāmaḥ bhavatām nu ayam ājñā garīyasī śambhoḥ na praveśaḥ kaścana pratiṣiddhān katham brahman niyuktāḥ rakṣaṇāya ca īśa ājñām samatikramya prayacchāma praveśanam ekāntam prayayau brahmā lajjayā parayā anvitaḥ devāḥ sarve tatas dṛṣṭvā vrīḍā yuktam pitāmaham kruddhāḥ manyu parīta aṅgāḥ vākyam ūcuḥ gaṇeśvaram na evam tvām vibruvantam hi kṣaṃsyate vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ avamānam punar mā evam tvam asmākam kṛthāḥ gaṇa mā adya te vayam udyuktāḥ nāśayiṣyāma jīvitam guravaḥ kim bhavantaḥ me yena śaṃke aamara adhipāḥ prasādya śaṃkaram yūyam gatāḥ deva tvam uttamam gaṇa bhūtāḥ sureśasya yūyam api amara uttamāḥ vayam api atiyogi īśāḥ gaṇapāḥ tasya śūlinaḥ samānāḥ yūyam asmākam hīnāḥ vā tridivaukasaḥ yasmāt vayam sureśānam paśyāmaḥ ca sarvaiḥ eva suraiḥ tasmāt vayam pūjyāḥ sadā adhikāḥ paśyāmaḥ nirbhayāḥ yūyam praveśam kuruta amarāḥ yāvat adya gaṇeśānām balam drakṣyatha dāruṇam yat vā balam ca yogaḥ ca yat tapaḥ yaḥ ca vaḥ śramaḥ pragṛhīta āyudhāḥ kṣipram śaṃkukarṇam vidudruvuḥ teṣām āpatatām vyāsa surāṇām śūra māninām na aśaknuvan padam dātum tasthuḥ stambhāḥ iva acalāḥ nigṛhīteṣu deveṣu viṣṇuḥ praharatām varaḥ abhidudrāva vegena śaṃkukarṇam gaṇeśvaram sa yogam punar āsthāya viṣṇuḥ aiśvarya saṃyutaḥ vinigṛhya sa tam viṣṇum gaṇeśaḥ śarva vallabhaḥ sa bhūtāni samutsṛjya tanmātrāṇi āviśat prabhuḥ indriyāṇi atha tattvāni bhāvān ca eva pitāmahaḥ apaśyat sthūla śirasam śaṃku karṇam mahā balam samāptam ca abhavat vyāsa vratam tasya mahātmanaḥ saḥ aapaśyat nigṛhītān tān sarvān eva divaukasaḥ sāmnā eva sa tadā devaḥ śaṃkukarṇam nyavārayat samyak ājñā kṛtā vatsa tvayā me gaṇapa īśvara muñca devān imān sarvān mā avamaṃsthāḥ gaṇeśvara evam devena samproktaḥ śaṃkukarṇaḥ gaṇādhipaḥ surāḥ api tadā sarve vrīḍayā parayā yutāḥ tān tadā vrīḍitān devaḥ prahasya vidita ātmavān uvāca brahmaṇā sārdham viṣṇum ābhāṣya su svaram gaṇeśvaraḥ deva īśāḥ mad śāsana paraḥ sadā yat anena kṛtam kiṃcid vyetu tat vaḥ sura uttamāḥ kartavyam yat ca tat brūta yad artham yūyam āgatāḥ śaṃkukarṇaḥ hi ayam śrīmān gaṇeśaḥ yena naḥ balam asmabhyam paratas dadyāḥ sadā darśanam ātmanaḥ śaṃkukarṇam iti khyātam tathā sthūleśvaram śubham yaḥ a atra āgatya carum kṛtvā mām yatnena arcayiṣyati sa yogam paramam prāpya śaṃku karṇa samaḥ bhavet yaḥ ca prāṇān iha kṣetre tyajet niyamam āsthitaḥ yūyam ca aiśvarya sampannāḥ tapaḥ yoga bala anvitāḥ nityam drakṣyatha mām devāḥ prayāta vigata jvarāḥ tatra liṅgam pratiṣṭhāpya cakre tīrthāni anekaśas surāṇām ca eva tīrthāni kṛtvā sva bhavanam yayau tasmin gatvā vane vyāsa hiraṇākṣaḥ pratāpavān ramyam himagireḥ śṛṅgam mainākam iti viśrutam tasya āgatya tadā devīm saṃsthitā pṛṣṭhatas śanais karābhyām padma tulyābhyām netre śambhoḥ nyamīlayat jānīte nā iti saṃcintya krīḍā artham himavat sutā apradṛśyam hi tamasā tathābhūtam purā yathā atha devaḥ tadā dṛṣṭvā tṛtīyam netram avyayam pārvatī pitaram dṛṣṭvā tathā dagdham pinākinā apanīya karau tasthau viṣaṇnā pati pārśvataḥ atha devaḥ tadā dṛṣṭvā viṣaṇṇa vadanām umām sa tathā dṛṣṭa mātraḥ tu punar paurāṇikaḥ abhavat prakṛti stham punar dṛṣṭvā devī pitaram avyayam praṇamya uvāca bhartāram hṛṣita āsyā girīndrajā śailaḥ eṣa tvayā deva kimartham bhasmasātkṛtaḥ andhāḥ samabhavat lokāḥ tadā sarve tamaḥ vṛtāḥ prakāśa artham tatas devi netram etat mayā kṛtam tvām ca dīnām pitṛ vadhāt upalakṣya tatas śubhe praiya artham tava suśroṇi prakṛti sthaḥ punar kṛtaḥ asmin ca tamasā graste loke tīvreṇa sarvatas tapyamānāya dāsyāmi hiraṇyākṣāya tam sutam sindhu sāgaram āgamya hiraṇyākṣam uvāca ha paśya mām tvam diteḥ putra cakṣuḥ divyam dadāmi te sa protthāya hiraṇyākṣaḥ praṇamya śirasā bhavam tam uvāca suraśreṣṭhaḥ śirasā pādayoḥ natam ayam te a asura śārdūla putraḥ putra śata agrajaḥ jagāma śikharam ramyam mainākam punar eva tu ādāya andham punar saḥ hiraṇyākṣaḥ mahā asuraḥ svam aśmakapuram yātvā bhāryām āha manasvinīm saṃvarddhaya enam yogena mā pramādam kṛthāḥ kvacid tasyāḥ snehena tam dṛṣṭvā stanābhyām prasnutam payaḥ dhanyāḥ te puruṣāḥ loke ye svayam yānti aviklavāḥ dṛṣṭvā dṛṣṭvā mukhān bandhūn ābhāṣya hi yathāsukham kāryam bhavet ca loṣṭena tṛṇena atha śavena vā na mayā anugṛhītena dhik mām aśubha kāriṇam suta snehāt ayam daityaḥ sadā mām rakṣate pitā hantavyam na vihanti eṣa koṣa kṣaya karam hi mām na ayam snehena saṃmūḍhaḥ jānāti imām mama vyathām āmantrya sahitam mātrā pitaram tam praṇamya ca pitrā śoka paripluta īkṣaṇa mukhena āyāsa khinna ātmanā tad tīvra ādhṛti kheda dṛṣṭi viraha prakṣveḍitaḥ śvasan tapaḥprayāṇam nāma gaṅgādvāram śubha dvāram tapovanam anuttamam vṛkṣāḥ kanakhalāḥ yatra sarve eva hi kāñcanāḥ sarvān tān śaraṇam yāmi tapaḥ siddhim diśantu me varān yena āpnuyām iṣṭān mṛtyum vā prāṇa nāśanam tathā triṣavaṇa snāyī juhvānaḥ a agnīn tathā eva ca varṣe varṣe tu sampūrṇe sva aṅgāt utkṛṣya kārṣikam piśitasya tadā bhāgam juhāva agnau mahā asuraḥ juhvataḥ svāni māṃsāni varṣāṇām pratyaham tadā saḥ a asuraḥ śaṃkha hāra ambu sudhā kunda indu pāṇḍaraḥ babhūva asthi sirā śeṣaḥ kevalam tapasi sthitaḥ sa tena tapasā yuktaḥ bhāskaram niṣprabham tadā somam ca graha tārābhiḥ saha cakre a amita ātmavān aśoṣayat palvalāni nadī kūpa sarāṃsi ca tasya tat tapasaḥ rūpam dṛṣṭvā vighna śatāni uta tuṣṭaḥ aasmi te mahā bāho cakṣuṣmān bhava suvrata śarīram prakṛtim yātu yathāpūrvam ca te a asura brūhi ca anyam varam bhūyas yaḥ te manasi saṃsthitaḥ sa cakṣuṣmān tadā bhūtvā śarīreṇa akṣatena ca mahā bala tvam māyāḥ ca tathā ca amara tām api hiraṇyākṣa sutam prāha punar eva vacaḥ śubham yā strī syāt loka mātā vai praṇameyam na tām yadi jagāma svam tadā āvāsam virarāma andhakaḥ ca saḥ sa gaṅgā ambhobhiḥ āplutya devam iṣṭvā yathāvidhi sa hṛṣṭa cetāḥ tapasā mahā balaḥ vivṛddha kāmaḥ sura śatru toṣaṇaḥ pituḥ sakāśam punar eva dīptimān agacchat hūta balāhaka upamaḥ saḥ a aṣṭābhiḥ dānavaiḥ sārdham gatvā tat puram antike nivedayāmāsa pituḥ kṛtārthaḥ a andhakaḥ āgataḥ priya ākhyātuḥ tatas vyāsa hiraṇyākṣaḥ a asura īśvaraḥ dadau ratnāni anekāni tathā kāmān abhīpsitān svayam rājā tadā sarvaiḥ suhṛdbhiḥ saha vāhanaiḥ saha strībhiḥ yayau tūrṇam somaḥ tārā gaṇaiḥ iva āsasāda sutam śrīmān somaḥ budham iva aparam kṛtārtham sa sutam dṛṣṭvā tuṣṭaḥ aabhūt dānava īśvaraḥ śrutvā te a asura śārdūlāḥ praṇeduḥ sāgarāḥ iva sa putra sahitaḥ bhāti daityaḥ sampūrṇa mānasaḥ caran rāśim iva ādityaḥ yathā saha śanaiscaraḥ adhvaryuḥ iva sānnāyye yathā anyān ṛtvijaḥ sthitān hiraṇyākṣaḥ uvāca śṛṇudhvam yat idānīm me kṛtyam hṛdi cira sthitam mayā manīṣitam pūrvam kṛtakṛtyaḥ iha āgate kariṣyāmi iti tat sarvam kriyatām aviśaṅkayā ghuṣyatām nagara hi asmin catvareṣu sabhāsu ca kaumudī divasān aṣṭau sapta ca eva mama ājñayā asmad pakṣe ca ye viprāḥ śukra prabhṛtayaḥ śubhāḥ te evam uktāḥ daitya indrāḥ hiraṇyākṣeṇa dhīmatā tathā anyeṣām ca pakṣāṇām ātmanaḥ śakti nandana suhṛd vargaiḥ tu sahitāḥ gacchadhvam tam didṛkṣavaḥ ghaṇṭikaḥ ca ahataiḥ vastraiḥ veṣṭitaḥ su vibhūṣitaḥ ārūḍhaḥ pāṇḍaram mattam gajam samavaghoṣayat hatāḥ daitya dviṣaḥ sarve danavānām ca śatravaḥ jayāya diti putrāṇām ādityānām bhayāya ca prātar prabhṛti rājā vaḥ ājñāpayati kaumudīm su saṃmṛṣṭa upaliptābhiḥ rathyābhiḥ kriyatām puram sragviṇaḥ sa śiraḥsnātāḥ datta bhuktāḥ yathākramam gāyantu gāyanāḥ ca eva nṛtyantu naṭa nartakāḥ ucchriyantām patākāḥ ca gṛha vīthi āpaṇeṣu ca puṣpa prakara juṣṭāni dhūpaiḥ nānāvidhaiḥ api sragdāmavanti sarvāṇi vanamālā ākulāni ca brāhmaṇāḥ ca eva bhojyantām kriyantām vācāni ca brahmaghoṣāḥ ca sarvatra puṇya aha udīraṇāni ca dīpa rātrau ca sarvatra rājamārge gṛheṣu ca taruṇāḥ saha yoṣidbhiḥ samantāt paryaṭantu ca ramamāṇāḥ hasantaḥ ca gāyantaḥ nṛtta sevinaḥ bhāṇḍa vādyāni vādyantām nṛtyantām daitya yoṣitaḥ mahādevasya pūjām ca gandha puṣpa ādikīm śubhām upahārān ca vividhān snapanāni mahānti ca valayaḥ puṣkalāḥ ca eva bhakṣya bhojyaiḥ alaṃkṛtāḥ dīpān ca vividha ākārān pānāni ca rasān tathā phalāni ca vicitrāṇi māṃsa pakvam anūpakam suvarṇa maṇi ctrāṇi īśvarāya prayacchata vaddhyantām paśavaḥ ca atra bhojyantām dvijasattamāḥ yaḥ na kuryāt idam sarvam pura vāsī janaḥ kvacid pātayet tasya śārīram rājā daṇḍam mahā yaśāḥ evam sa ghoṣayitvā tu ghāṇṭikaḥ hasti pṛṣṭhatas jagāma sva gṛham bhūyas kaumudī ca abhyavartata kṣīreṇa sarpiṣā dadhnā rasaiḥ ca bahubhiḥ śubhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ phalaiḥ ca bījaiḥ ca ratnaiḥ ca adbhiḥ samanvitaiḥ ekaikasya svayam rājā ghaṭānām daśabhiḥ śataiḥ pūjayitvā tatas rājā viprān anyān abhojayat bali śayyā āsana ādyaiḥ ca stūyamānaḥ gṛhān yayau cakruḥ sarve muni śreṣṭha yathā rājā tathā eva te eṣa devaḥ sadā putra tava astu sumukhaḥ prabhuḥ asmin bhaktiḥ tvayā kāryā bhāvitā parameśvare eṣa vandyaḥ ca pūjyaḥ ca atas na asti paraḥ guruḥ saḥ evam uktvā praṇipatya śaṃkaram mahā asuraḥ saḥ a asura dānava īśvaraḥ atha tasyām saha strībhiḥ kaumudyām daitya dānavāḥ tatra kācid śubha apāṅgī padmam divyam mukha upari mukha aravinda trāṇa artham dadau soma bhayāt iva aparā caraṇena etya priyam matā abhyatāḍayat śītāṃśuḥ iva pādena vikacam śarad ambujam aparāḥ rājamārgāṇām anabhijñāḥ kulastriyaḥ sphāṭika upacitām bhūmim dṛṣṭvā muñcanti pādukām utkṛṣya vasanam ca anyā vipra indra jala śaṅkayā rājamārgeṣu gacchanti dṛṣṭvā maṇi citām bhuvam gṛhāt candra aṃśubhiḥ spṛṣṭā papāta madana alasā patitā eva ca sā somam uvāca idam kula aṅganā bhūyas mām tvam karaiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ dahase nalinīm iva sūryeṇa kila dagdhāni jīvayasi auṣadhāni ha madanena katham dagdhām na jīvayasi mām dṛḍham jala candre iva ābhānti vīci cañcala mūrtayaḥ kecid gāyanti sahitāḥ strībhiḥ daityāḥ mahā balāḥ rājamārgaḥ kvacid bhāti strībhiḥ bahvībhiḥ āvṛtaḥ kvacid nūpura kāñcīnām kokilānām ca nisvanaḥ babhau śrotra sukhaḥ śrotuḥ kalā madana dīpanaḥ kvacid ramaṇa raktānām strīṇām vyāsa priyaiḥ saha hṛdaya vyākula karaḥ śrūyate madhuraḥ svaraḥ dānavānām tadā prīti saukhya viśrambha vardhanaḥ prakṛti sthe jane vyāsa dānavāḥ te samāgatāḥ viviśuḥ bhīma saṃhlādāḥ sabhām divyām manoramām muktā dāma avalamba ādhyām sraj dāma uttama bhūṣitām divya jāla upasannaddhām kiṅkiṇī citra śobhitām upetām dīrghikābhiḥ ca bahu śayyā āsanām śubhām aklamām bahu nisyandām sarva duḥkha vivarjitām sarva kāma samṛddhām ca bahu strī puruṣa ākulām viviśuḥ tām sabhām divyām daityāḥ darpa samāvṛtāḥ virocanaḥ baliḥ ca eva bali putrāḥ ca viśrutāḥ sahasrāṇi daśa dve ca yeṣām śreṣṭhaḥ mahā balaḥ bāṇaḥ nāma mahā māyaḥ rājā bāhu sahasradhṛk bāṇasya putrāḥ ye ca anye sahasrāṇi daśa eva ca muraḥ dhamuṣumuṇḍaḥ ca vipracittiḥ ca dānavaḥ kālaḥ hayaśirāḥ ca eva sambaraḥ kālasambaraḥ viśrutaḥ ca indradamanaḥ kālanemī ca dānavaḥ rāhuḥ ca śataketuḥ ca dhvajaḥ tāraḥ sa tārakaḥ andhakaḥ ca tathā aṭiḥ ca bakaḥ śarabhaḥ eva ca mahākāyaḥ śatānandaḥ śatadundubhiḥ eva ca kaṇḍalaḥ ca eva mīḍhaḥ ca durlomā ca eva viśrutaḥ dhundhuḥ ca śatamāyaḥ ca śatavārttaḥ a atha duḥsahaḥ mayaḥ ca dānava śreṣṭhaḥ maya putrāḥ ca viśrutāḥ teṣām aṣṭau sahasrāṇi sarve ca kṛta niśacayāḥ vṛtraḥ ca dānava śreṣṭhaḥ namuciḥ bhajahaḥ tathā śatodaraḥ ca vyaṃsaḥ ca tāthāyaḥ ca hradodaraḥ hālaḥ gārgyaśirāḥ ca eva kuśaḥ śambhuḥ tathā eva ca tathā gaganamūrdhā ca sālukaḥ śālvaḥ eva ca kharaḥ khararomā ca sūcīlomā ca dānavaḥ parighaḥ aaparighaḥ ca eva daṇḍaḥ kāśyapaḥ eva ca dundubhiḥ ca mahā kāyaḥ saiṃhikeyaḥ ca yaḥ gaṇaḥ paulomāḥ ṣaṣṭi sāhasrāḥ kālakeyāḥ ca viśrutāḥ kālakañjāḥ sahasrāṇi aṣṭau te devakaṇṭakāḥ nivāta kavacānām ca tisraḥ koṭyaḥ mahā balāḥ tāḥ eva gaṇitāḥ sarvāḥ koṭyaḥ ṣaṣṭiḥ suradviṣām tad pakṣān varjayitvā tu manuṣyān pannagān api rākṣasān ca piśācān ca yakṣān pakṣinaḥ eva ca teṣām api tadā vyāsa koṭyaḥ viṃśati saṃmitāḥ te tām sabhām samāviśya sarve darpāt suradviṣaḥ sa vidyut ulkāḥ bhānti sma prāvṛṣ jaladharāḥ iva te svanantaḥ iva ambhodāḥ sa dhūmāḥ iva parvatāḥ utpātāḥ iva nirmuktāḥ sa viṣāḥ iva pannagāḥ gajāḥ iva ca saṃmattāḥ pavanāḥ iva vegitāḥ surāṇām viprakāra artham tasthuḥ āpūrya tām sabhām sukha āsīnam tadā vyāsa siṃhāsana gatam yuvā baddhāñjali puṭaḥ samyak vācā ślakṣaṇa pada akṣaram prahlāda ādyāḥ sabhām sarve vipracitti purogamāḥ tatas suradviṣaḥ śrutvā sarvān prāptān sabhām tadā sa kirīṭī mahā daṃṣṭraḥ muñja keśaḥ mahā hanuḥ ākarṇāt dārita āsyaḥ ca mahā chatra śirāḥ tathā ājānu bāhuḥ vṛtta akṣaḥ piṅga akṣaḥ ca ūrddhva romavān hāra varya aṅkita uraskaḥ sa balākaḥ iva ambudaḥ sa tām tadā dānava rāja sattamaḥ vicitra ratna aṃśu parīta kuṇḍalaḥ sabhām viveśa asura daitya puṅgavaiḥ samāvṛtām dyām uditaiḥ grahaiḥ iva hiraṇyākṣasya sabhāpraveśaḥ nāma sa gatvā tām sabhām vyāsa apaśyat daitya dānavān prāvṛṣ kāle mattān yathā indraḥ samupasthitān mā uttiṣṭhata iti tān sarvān avocat asura adhipaḥ māyā pañcakam āsādya bhogī vāyudharān iva sa teṣām agratas bhāti hiraṇya akṣaḥ mahā asuraḥ madhye kalabha vṛndānām yathā mattaḥ mahā gajaḥ bhāti siṃhāsana āsīnaḥ sa rājā sura vidviṣām meru śṛṅgam iva āsādya sa balākaḥ mahā ambudaḥ babhūvuḥ upaviṣṭāḥ ca tūṣṇīm eva asurāḥ tadā sisṛkṣavaḥ jalam kāle nīla ambhodāḥ iva unnatāḥ sa teṣām iṅgita jñā kāla ca sura tāpana hiraṇyākṣaḥ svayam rājā tān uvāca suradviṣaḥ yūyam sarve mahā māyāḥ śṛṇudhvam saṃyatāḥ samam kāla prāptam ca pathyam ca śiṣyāḥ iva guroḥ vacaḥ mama bhrātā priyaḥ hi āsīt jyeṣṭhaḥ pūjyaḥ yathā pitā rājā mahātmā satya saṃgaraḥ dhārmikaḥ ca vadānyaḥ ca yajvā dātā tapaḥ nvitaḥ rājya arthe sa hataḥ devaiḥ vikṛtyā mūḍha mānasaiḥ tasya duḥkhena tapyāmi śuṣyāmi ca mahā asurāḥ salilāt uddhṛtam grīṣme padmam kusumitam yathā teṣām kartum aham daṇḍam śaktaḥ bhavatām tatra bālānām rakṣā artham na udyataḥ hi aham saḥ aaham daṇḍam svayam kartum ekākī asura vidviṣām gamiṣyāmi na saṃdehaḥ mā bhīḥ bhavatu vaḥ a asurāḥ yadi yuddhe na tiṣṭhanti māyām kāṃcid vinā āśritāḥ bālaiḥ api hiraṇyākṣa su upapattyā prasādhitam hiraṇya kaśipuu rājā bhrātā naḥ jyeṣṭhaḥ uttamaḥ ākrandantam vinā rājan atas vijñāpayāmi te bhavataḥ tu vayam bālāḥ na teṣām asura īśvara śaktāḥ vayam su yuddhe na tān hantum kula pāṃsanān tathā asmākam mahā rāja mā naḥ bālān yathā cara tariṣyāmaḥ vayam tat vai bāla matsyāḥ iva arṇavam prahlādaḥ a ayam saha bhrātā sa sutaḥ sa suhṛd janaḥ ekaḥ eva āhvayet sarvān yathā siṃhaḥ mahā dvipān surān sayama vitta īśān śaktaḥ sarvān prabādhitum ayam bāṇaḥ ca tad putraḥ rājā bāhu sahasradhṛk ayam mayaḥ ca vikrāntaḥ sārakaḥ tāraḥ eva ca anye ca dānavāḥ śūrāḥ śaktāḥ devān prabādhitum badhiṣyāmaḥ surān sarvān vyādhāḥ iva mṛgān vane śūraḥ aayam andhakaḥ śrīmān brahmadatta varaḥ tadā pūrvam devāḥ vayam sarve paścāt te devatāḥ abhavan katham bibhīma teṣām vai siṃhānām śarabhāḥ iva uvāca vacanam tatra harṣayan dānava īśvarān mahā balāḥ vayam rājan bala hīnāḥ surāḥ tu te adya mātā ditiḥ teṣām duḥkham tiṣṭhadhvam dānavāḥ sarve paryāptaḥ a aham durātmanām asmākam rāja hantR̥ṇām mṛtyuḥ asmi samāgataḥ uvāca dānavān vyāsa jaya īpsuḥ sura vidviṣām adya puṣyam ca nakṣatram asmākam bala kāraṇam adya eva daṃśitāḥ sarve drutam niryātu dānavāḥ atha te dānavāḥ śīghram vāhanāni mudā yutāḥ prāvārayanta saṃgrāme yogyāni ripu bādhane śīghram kurudhvam sajjāni gacchāmaḥ daivatān prati teṣām su tumulaḥ śabdaḥ vāhanānām babhūva ha mama aśvaḥ tava siṃhaḥ mama ayam gaja yūthapaḥ niryāntu daitya indrāḥ yāsyāmaḥ pṛṣṭhatas vayam āroha svam ratham vīra kim rathena mama adya vai padātiḥ eva saṃgrāme haṃsyāmi adya suradviṣaḥ evam teṣām abhūt śabdaḥ su manojñaḥ suradviṣām vaṃśa vīṇā svana unmiśraḥ pralāpaḥ iva yoṣitām atha śukraḥ tadā abhyetya bhṛgoḥ putraḥ mahā tapāḥ hiraṇyākṣam uvāca idam harṣayan dānava īśvaram mayā śāntiḥ kṛtā te a adya rakṣā ca saha dānavaiḥ aham tvā svena tapasā sahabhṛtyam sa bāndhavam dhārayiṣyāmi yudhyantam lokān yajñaḥ iva aparaḥ bhārgavam puratas kṛtvā nirjagāma sa mandirāt sāḍastamāḥ diṇḍimāḥ ca bhāṇḍāḥ ca paṭahaiḥ saha pañcākṣāḥ saridhīkāḥ ca mukundāḥ murajāḥ tathā divyābhiḥ kula vṛddhābhiḥ strībhiḥ ca kṛta maṅgalāḥ dvāri apaśyan ratham kḷptam yathā deva mayam haraḥ śata nalva pramāṇa aṅkam divyam jāmbūnadam dṛḍham putraḥ garutmanaḥ vyāsa khagaḥ kāma gamaḥ śubhaḥ tam asau rāja śabdena āśīrbhiḥ ca abhipūjitaḥ āruroha ratham rājā eka cakram yathā raviḥ baddha godhā aṅgulitraḥ ca kaṇṭha trāṇa kṛtaḥ tathā bahvībhiḥ vaijayantībhiḥ sa balākaḥ iva ambudaḥ tādṛś eva rathaḥ hi āsīt sutasya su mahātmanaḥ tri sāhasrān haya agryān tu yān rathe pratyacūcudat pracoditāḥ tam ūḍhuḥ te javanāḥ vāta raṃhasaḥ hayāḥ kāma gamāḥ sapta raveḥ iva mahā ratham tasya agratas tadā śukraḥ rathena ambuda nādinā dakṣiṇāyanam āsādya yathā lokasya bhāskaraḥ dvitīyam pārśvam āśritya vipracittiḥ abhūt sthitaḥ pṛṣṭhatas ca agratas ca eva yathāprīti yayuḥ tadā apare dānavāḥ śūrāḥ śaśāṅkasya yathā grahāḥ valgita āsphoṭita utkruṣṭam siṃhanāda uddhatam yayau bāhu śabda kucita avanāditaiḥ yāti dānava balam bhaya āvaham hiraṇyākṣaniryāṇam nāma kṛṣṇadvaipāyanaḥ uvāca katham antarhitam teṣām kaḥ mantraḥ ceṣṭitam ca kim dhārmikāḥ ca vadānyāḥ ca vidvāṃsaḥ chinna saṃśayāḥ iṣṭāḥ sarvasya lokasya kim akurvata devatāḥ śṛṇu vyāsa yathā devāḥ uttaram pratipedire asurāṇām ca yat yuktam pratyakurvata daṃśitāḥ udyukteṣu eva daityeṣu devānām bhaya sūcakāḥ saṃjajñire a amarāvatyām utpātāḥ dāruṇāḥ bhṛśam piśita āśāḥ khagāḥ tatra bhramanti upari sarvatas mamluḥ mālyāni devānām gandhaḥ ca eva aśubham vavau āyudhāni ca dīpyante sa dhūmam śaktinandana kabandhāḥ ca tathā āditye yuddham ca atra pradṛśyate dvayoḥ puruṣayoḥ vyāsa hāhākāraḥ ca sarvaśas devānām aratim ca eva cittam vā api samaśnute sphuranti vāmanetrāṇi jṛmbhikā ca upajāyate etān ca anyān ca vividhān utpātān bhaya sūcakān dṛṣṭvā prāha gurum śakraḥ bṛhaspatim idam vacaḥ yat ime sūcayanti ugrāḥ utpātāḥ naḥ su dāruṇāḥ na ime niṣkāraṇam manye na ca alpī alpam hi tat hi naḥ yat ime sūcayanti ugrāḥ kalpānte iva dāruṇāḥ nirīkṣya buddhyā saṃcintya provāca idam tatas vacaḥ yat bhavān āha tat tathyam na ime niṣkāraṇam tathā sa hataḥ narasiṃhena prāptam rājyam ca naḥ punar daitya adhipāḥ nirutsāhāḥ dānavaiḥ saha śerate pātāle danu putrāḥ ca duḥkha śoka samāhatāḥ andhakaḥ ca asya putraḥ brahmaṇā hi amaraḥ kṛtaḥ tat bhavantaḥ su saṃnaddhāḥ sarve yuddhāya daṃśitāḥ pratīkṣadhvam mahāsattvāḥ yāvat autpātikam sthitam uvāca sajjībhavata yuddhāya kṛta niścayāḥ bhayam mahat idam prāptam mā tiṣṭhata nirudyamāḥ mā naḥ samprāpya daitya indrāḥ hanyuḥ mattān iva amarāḥ eṣa daityaḥ hiraṇyākṣaḥ sarva asura samāvṛtaḥ tat bhavān daṃśitaḥ samyak saha asmābhiḥ mahā balaḥ tatas sa bhagavān vyāsa mahātmā devarāj svayam abhedyam kavacam divyam jāmbūnada vibhūṣitam tatas godhā aṅgulitrāṇi talatram ca eva bhāsuram iṣudhīn akṣayān ca eva khaṅgam ca eva amṛta udbhavam ābabaddha asya mantreṇa yat ajaryam śara ādibhiḥ tatas sa śaram cāpam pañca vajram su dāruṇam ghaṭam ca pūrṇam astram ca airāvatam atha api ca arcitam dhūpitam ca eva dhūpitam krodha darpaṇaiḥ āruḍhaḥ sa tadā bhāti airāvatagakam vibhuḥ pāṇḍareṇa ātapatreṇa surarāj sambabhau tadā haṃsa saṃvījitam phullam śaradi iva mahā utpalam tasya pārśve jayantaḥ ca putraḥ śakrasya dhārmikaḥ tatas yamaḥ ca puṇḍreṇa mahiṣeṇa mahā balaḥ śatāni ca tathā aśītiḥ tathā eva aṣṭa adhikā punar varuṇaḥ niryayau devaḥ putrāṇām daśabhiḥ śataiḥ yādobhiḥ te vṛtāḥ sarve koṭyaḥ tāḥ ca ekaviṃśatiḥ tatas vāyuḥ mahā tejāḥ sarva vyāpī mahā javaḥ ādityāḥ niryayuḥ sarve dvādaśa akṣauhiṇī vṛtāḥ tathā rudrāḥ ca nirjagmuḥ mahā māyā mahā balāḥ ekaikaśas tadā sarve ṣoḍaśa akṣauhiṇī vṛtāḥ vasavaḥ daśabhiḥ ca eva aṣṭābhiḥ ca aśvinau ubhau tathā eva aṅgirasaḥ devāḥ sarve te aakṣauhiṇī vṛtāḥ yāmāḥ dāmāḥ ca sādhyāḥ ca lekhāḥ ye ca mahā balāḥ te a api koṭī śataiḥ sarve daśabhiḥ saha saṃgatāḥ abhūvan viṃśatiḥ koṭyaḥ gandharvāṇām mahātmanām pannagānām catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ khagānām ca eva viṃśatiḥ piśācānām ca koṭyaḥ tāḥ pañcāśat tatra saṃgatāḥ rājñām saḥ dṛṣṭvā tat bahu aparyantam devānām balam uttamam bhagavan kriyatām vyūhaḥ yasmāt teṣām durātmanām adhṛṣyaḥ ca aprameyaḥ ca yat na vetti ca bhārgavaḥ na vetti yasya vā bhedam na kuryāt pākaśāsana uśanāḥ sa tu yat vetti mahā yogī mahā yaśāḥ sa tu tīkṣṇa tayā ca eva mānāt ca eva śacīpate yena tān dānavān saṃkhye kaṃcid kālam vijeṣyatha śyenam nāma mahā yogī devānām hitam ādadhat grīvāyām tasya saṃcakre vitteśam varuṇam tathā śiraḥ cūḍām yamam tasya mṛtyum kālam ca tau ubhau vaktram tasya akarot jīvaḥ candra sūryau ca cakṣuṣī ekam pakṣam cakāra asya rudrān sarvān sa vai dvijaḥ gandharvāḥ pannagāḥ ca eva nakhān tasya samāśritāḥ rājānaḥ tasya pāda sthāḥ tale bhūtān nyaveśayat mahā balān mahā utsāhān sarvān devān sabhāsadaḥ sa kṛtvā tam khaga vyūham mantreṇa eva abhimantrya ca uvāca devarājam tam bṛhaspatiḥ idam vacaḥ kalpitaḥ mayā vyūhaḥ kṛtvā rākṣasa pārthivam jaya śatrūn raṇe śakra devataiḥ abhirakṣitaḥ bhāgyāt naḥ jaya lābhāya na anyatra sura puṃgava jahi śatrūn mahā bāho mā bhīḥ bhavatu te raṇe āḍambarāḥ ca nambhāḥ ca kṛkavāḥ goviṣāṇakāḥ mardalāḥ pepukāḥ citrāḥ neduḥ tūryāṇi sarvaśas tena śabdena yodhānām yuddha harṣaḥ mahān abhūt siṃhanādān akurvan te vādita āsphoṭitāni ca devāḥ tatas śaṅkha mṛdaṅga vādyaiḥ amogha sarva āyudha dīpta hastāḥ vyūḍhe surāṇām sainye a atha sthite yuddhāya daṃśite siṃhanādān akurvan te vāditra rava miśritān sa nādaḥ su mahān vyāsa sāgarasya iva mathyataḥ āpūrayat jagat sarvam yugānta ambuda nisvanaḥ tatas dānava śārdūlāḥ sarve dṛṣṭvā sura uttamān vyūhya sainyam sthitān samyak vīkṣāṃcakruḥ parasparam tān vīkṣamāṇān saṃdṛśya sthitaḥ dānava yūthapān uvāca bhārgavam vyāsa hiraṇyanayanaḥ svayam ete vyūham imam kṛtvā devāḥ tiṣṭhanti abhīta vat manyante dānavān yuddhe vayam jeṣyāma sarvaśas khagasya tasya yat chettum śiraḥ kāyāt iyeṣa ca cakāra tasya śṛṅge tu prahlāda andhaka dānavau dhārān nivāta kavacān paulomān kālakā sutān pṛṣṭham cakre mahā yogī madhyam rājānam eva ca uvāca ca enam viśrabdhaḥ bhārgavaḥ śatru tāpanaḥ hiraṇyākṣam idam yuktam kāle pathyam ca sarvaśas daivata vyūha bhettā ayam mahā vyūhaḥ hi vaḥ kṛtaḥ yuktam kuruta bhadram vaḥ mayā anuparipālitāḥ mahā māyāḥ surāḥ sarve yathā na eva abhimanyata āḍambarāḥ ḍiṇḍimāḥ ca paṭahāḥ ca mahā svanāḥ devānām dānavānām ca mada sattva pradāni vai mamarduḥ devatā anīkam prahatāḥ sarvaśas tathā devāḥ te ca saṃyattāḥ tapaḥ yoga bala anvitāḥ dānavān vividhaiḥ śastraiḥ abhijaghnuḥ kṛta āgasaḥ asīnām pātyamānānām śirasām hastinām api āsīt śabdaḥ mahā ghoraḥ vaṃśānām iva pāṭyatām vikṛṣyatām ca dhanuṣām bāṇānām ca vimucyatām ākāśam abhavat sarvam khadyotaiḥ iva saṃvṛtam kṣaṇena yuddha bhūmiḥ sā viṣamā samapadyata kabandhaiḥ ca abhinṛtyadbhiḥ durnirīkṣyam abhūt raṇe niṣka sūtraiḥ su mukuṭaiḥ keyūraiḥ kuṇḍalaiḥ api kaṇṭha trāṇaiḥ vicitraiḥ ca dukūlaiḥ maṇibhiḥ ca ha muśalaiḥ śūla daṇḍaiḥ ca ghaṇṭābhiḥ ca aṅkuśaiḥ api kruddhaḥ keśeṣu saṃgṛhya virathaḥ aapi mahā rathaḥ ratham cikṣepa ca vṛtaiḥ kuśa muṣṭim iva adhvare hatam api eṣa turagaḥ sādinam patitam kṣitau upāste pādataḥ suptam gurum śiṣyaḥ yathā ātmavān vahanti puruṣāḥ kṣīvāḥ jñānavantaḥ iva āgamān bāhubhyām veṣṭayitvā tam prayayau devatā antikam sa tena eva gajena urvyām papāta viniṣūditaḥ ete padātayaḥ ca anye bahavaḥ saṃhatāḥ bhṛśam evam ākāśa gāḥ siddhāḥ ṛṣayaḥ cāraṇāḥ tathā piśācāḥ ca api modante nṛtyanti ca nikumbhilāḥ jaghnuḥ tadā rākṣasa tulya rūpāḥ svārājya hetoḥ jayam īpsamānāḥ mṛtyuḥ kālaḥ yamaḥ ca eva kṛtāntaḥ kalahaḥ tathā nikṛtiḥ vyādhayaḥ ca eva vairam krodhaḥ ca vīryavān narakāḥ ca eva te sarve yamadūtāḥ ca sarvaśas pretāḥ ca pitaraḥ ca eva catvāraḥ ye gaṇāḥ tu iti vāmanaḥ ca eva yaḥ devāḥ kiṃkarāḥ ca mahā balāḥ niṣṭananti kṣitau daityāḥ nirayeṣu iva pāpinaḥ asi carma viniṣpiṣṭāḥ mṛtyu pāśa uvapāśitāḥ kṛtānta vaśa gāḥ sarve sambhajyanti diteḥ sutāḥ tatas hāhākṛtaḥ śabdaḥ dānavānām bale abhavat sa śaraiḥ bahubhiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ akuṇṭha agraiḥ ayaḥ mayaiḥ varuṇam ca dhaneśam ca vāyum ca eva abhyavarṣata sa saṃchādya tadā bhāsam bhāskarasya śitaiḥ karaiḥ avākirat tadā devān saṃvarttakaḥ iva ambudaḥ te śara arcita sarva aṅgāḥ vadhyamānāḥ punar punar yāntam āyāntam aprāptam prāptam dūra stham eva ca athā evam asyataḥ vyāsa varuṇaḥ kārmukam tadā tam tathā nikṛtam dṛṣṭvā baliḥ bāṇaḥ ca tau ubhau prahlādaḥ bhrātṛbhiḥ sārdham lokapālān upādravan teṣām āpatatām eva lokapālāḥ mahā balāḥ vāyuḥ bāṇasya viśikhaiḥ kārmukāṇi vikarṣataḥ cikṣepa tāni vegena vāyuḥ anyām diśam punar gadām gṛhītvā vegena śvasanam samupādravat śaraiḥ āśīviṣa ākāraiḥ gadām tām samakṛntata sa nikṛtta gadaḥ ca api bāṇaḥ praharatām varaḥ pragṛhya tu gadām anyām cikṣepa ca jahāsa ca tām āpatantīm vegena gadām bāṇasya khecarīm ākṣipya vāyuḥ vyadhamat anyām diśam adīna vat viratham tam atha ālakṣya vāyunā ca bhṛśa arditam sūryācandramasau yadvat pūrṇamāsyām samāgatau atha teṣām ca teṣām ca tat yuddham abhavat tadā tān niruddhān atha ājñāya lokapālaiḥ mahā balaiḥ paulomāḥ saiṃhikeyāḥ ca śambaraḥ kālaśambaraḥ andhakaḥ ca mahā daityaḥ hiraṇyākṣaḥ ca viśrutaḥ sarve vyūham a muñcantaḥ dānavāḥ yuddha lālasāḥ cakruḥ vegam mahā vegāḥ sa meghāḥ iva sānavaḥ tān āpatataḥ eva atha yamaḥ daṇḍena vīryavān avārayata te tasthuḥ citra paṭṭe iva arpitāḥ tān yamasya bhayāt daityān dṛṣṭvā sarvān avasthitān yamam lalāṭe daśabhiḥ bāṇaiḥ vivyādha vīryavān pañcabhiḥ mahiṣam ca asya ṣaḍbhiḥ kālam tathā agratas mṛtyum ca daśabhiḥ ca eva kṛtāntam ca eva saptabhiḥ hiraṇyākṣa sutaḥ jyeṣṭhaḥ hi andhakaḥ aham mahā balaḥ tasya tām daṇḍa ghātena gadām saḥ śirasi enam punar kruddhaḥ daṇḍena samatāḍayat puras pratihatam dṛṣṭvā jahasuḥ dānava īśvarāḥ siṃhanādān sa vāditrān bāhuśabdān ca su svarān talena āhatya mahiṣāt yamam bhūmau nyapātayat tatas hāhākṛtam devaiḥ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa iti dānavaiḥ atha mṛtyuḥ ca kālaḥ ca kṛtāntaḥ ca iti te trayaḥ andhakam bahubhiḥ pāśaiḥ babandhuḥ krodha mūrcchitāḥ pāśān ca eva sa daitya indraḥ vicchidya su mahā balaḥ bāhu pāśena balavān kṛtāntam avabadhnata sa baddhaḥ bāhu pāśena kṛtāntaḥ krodha mūrcchitaḥ utkṣipya andhakam uddhūtaḥ nināya balavān balāt tam nīyamānam samprekṣya hiraṇāykṣaḥ sutam svakam jaghāna sāyakaiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ kṛtāntam su bhṛśam dṛḍham tasya gātrāṇi sarvāṇi sandhīn ca eva mahā balaḥ tam bāṇaiḥ vivṛtam dṛṣṭvā devāḥ sarve sa vāsavāḥ atha ki khara mṛdaṅga veṇu vīṇā paṇava sughoṣaka ḍiṇḍi mānakānām asura sura bale mahān tadānīm samabhavat ugrataraḥ sa tūrya nādaḥ hiraṇyākṣaḥ tadā āyāntam dṛṣṭvā devendram agratas anayat tam sura indrāya hutam bhāgam yathā analaḥ śarapāte tadā sthitvā provāca asura puṅgavaḥ pūrvadevāḥ vayam ca eva trailokyam ca akhilam hi naḥ jyeṣṭha krama āgatam kṛtvā nyāyena ca surottama te yūyam nyāyam utsṛjya hatvā me bhrātaram punar viśrambheṇa śaṭāḥ bhūtvā paścāt deva tvam āgatāḥ ime vayam pūrvadevāḥ navāḥ yūyam ca devatāḥ yudhyatām vedmi sarveṣām jīvitāni harāmi aham saha anugaiḥ tvām iha adya hatvā kṛtvā balim tathā bhaviṣyāmi anṛṇaḥ bhrātuḥ yaḥ tvayā māyayā hataḥ saḥ evam uktvā balavat dhanuḥ ākṛṣya vīryavān sa viddhaḥ tena devānām lalāṭe pañcabhiḥ śaraiḥ rājā babhau yathā meruḥ sūrya pādaiḥ samāvṛtaḥ sa tena su prahāreṇa diśaḥ dīptāḥ hi apaśyataḥ atha śaktim samādāya cikṣepa ruṣitaḥ tadā tām āpatantīm dīpta āsyām ugrām mṛtyoḥ iva svasām gadām anyām samudyamya hiraṇyākṣaḥ tadā śubhām ciccheda bahudhā rājā dharma arthau iva nāstikaḥ kālanemiḥ ca ṣaṇḍaḥ ca vipākaḥ pākaḥ eva ca tān āpatataḥ eva atha rudra ādityāḥ pramanyavaḥ rurudhuḥ vijane yadvat gajān siṃhāḥ samūrjitāḥ araṇye a anyonya sampātāt mattānām dantinām iva tatra aṃśaḥ nāma ādityaḥ rathena ambuda nādinā āyamya kārmukam vyāsa dānavān samupādravat sa vipracittim daśabhiḥ śinim pañcabhiḥ eva ca tribhiḥ ca vāṣkalim saṃkhye mayam pañcabhiḥ eva ca saṃvaram ca eva viṃśatyāḥ asilomānam eva ca tāram ca tārakam ca eva ilvalam namucim tathā vātāpim ca indradamanam vipākam pākam eva ca kālanemim ca ṣaṇḍam ca tathā cāmīsṛ muñjikam vikarṣatām ca saṃgrāme tatas eṣām punar vāhān sūtān divyān jaya dhvajān te hata aśvāḥ mahā iṣvāsāḥ hata sūtāḥ ca sarvaśas vegena khaṅginaḥ sarve aṃśam eva abhidudruvuḥ tān mūrttīn iva śaila indrān garjataḥ jaladān iva asambhrāntaḥ tat ādityaḥ jānuṣu eva avapūrayat śara pūrita gulphāḥ te jānuṣu eva ca dānavāḥ aṃśam utsṛjya vegena jagmuḥ anyām diśam tadā apātayat phalāni iva pakvāni śvasataḥ yathā dhāvanti kecid aṃśena aśiraskāḥ śaraiḥ kṛtāḥ gatvā vegena saṃgrāmāt patanti ūrvyām gatāsavaḥ apare bāhubhiḥ chinnaiḥ ūrubhiḥ ca mahā balāḥ patitāḥ niṣṭananti ugram niraya sthāḥ yathā narāḥ śara pūrita sarva aṅgāḥ śara sambaddha bandhanāḥ na sa tatra asti daityaḥ vā dānavaḥ vā mahā balaḥ yam śarāḥ na viśanti ugrāḥ aṃśasya su mahātmanaḥ aśoka kiṃśukānām vā tathā daitya balam babhau ayam aṃśaḥ na iti ayam ca ayam iti uta sarvam aṃśa mayam daityāḥ hi apaśyanta bhaya arditāḥ saḥ a api nitya āyata dhanuḥ pariveṣī iva bhāskaraḥ sa divya cāpaḥ laghu hastavān balī sadā mahā maṇḍala bhūta kārmukaḥ dadāha śatrūn pavana īritaḥ raṇe vibhāvasuḥ śuṣkam iva indhanam mahat tān tathā aṃśena daitya indrān kālyamānān itastatas dṛṣṭvā balaḥ mahā māyaḥ yantāram idam abravīt nayasva mām yatas a aṃśaḥ a ayam durātmā dahate balam tasya apatata eva aṃśaḥ tadā praharatām varaḥ dhvajam ciccheda bāṇena hayān ca ekaikaśas tadā bhallena ca śiraḥ kāyāt yantuḥ āśu nyapātayat tasya daityasya te bhānti lalāṭe pañca sāyakāḥ su vṛṣṭim iva jīmūtaḥ iva aśanim karān divākaraḥ yadvat tuṣāram candramāḥ yathā aṃśam eva akirat tīkṣṇaiḥ sāyakaiḥ marma bhedibhiḥ hayān vega hatān kṛtvā sārathim ca api asārathim yathāṃśam sa cakāra ugra sthān yān api daivatān nirudyamān mahā māyaḥ cakāra virarāsa ca kvacid gīṣpati saṃkāśaḥ kvacid bhāskara saṃnibhaḥ kvacid ca ākāśam āviśya adṛśyaḥ saḥ aasṛjat śarān viṣṭabdham mūka vat sarvam devānām balam ābabhau naṣṭa āvāditra ninadam haya heṣita varjitam gaja bṛṃhita vimraṣṭam śrānta yodha ugra nāditam ceṣṭā virahitāḥ ca āsan yathā mokṣa pathi sthitāḥ tān tathā sīdataḥ sarvān dṛṣṭvā bala mahā arṇave vāyuḥ jayantaḥ pūṣā ca bhagaḥ ca su mahā balāḥ vāyuḥ tasya mahābhāga śarān meghāt iva uditān muñcataḥ mucyamānān ca muktān ca eva aharat balī dehatrāṇam tathā pūṣā jayantaḥ ca iṣudhī tathā samārdayan śaraiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ devāḥ ravi sama prabhāḥ saḥ a ardyamānaḥ tadā devaiḥ balī dānava puṅgavaḥ vṛkṣaiḥ kānana jaiḥ vyāsa yuyudhe bhīma vikramaḥ tān drumān te tad ākṣiptān balena bahudhā śubhān gṛhītvā sa mahat śṛṅgam sa mṛga vyāla kinnaram cikṣepa su mahā nādam prakurvāṇaḥ jahāra śṛṅgam tat ca api papāta asura mūrdhani dānavānām tadā koṭim ekām vīryavatām yudhi jaghāna bala muktam tat giri śṛṅgam mahā svanam tān hatān dānavāḥ tena śṛṅgena diti nandanān dṛṣṭvā devān ca tān ugrān abhajyanta sahasraśas utsṛjya vāhanāni anye jīvita abhyarthinaḥ tadā pramuñcantaḥ mahā nādān palāyanta sahasrśas dravataḥ tān tadā abhyetya hiraṇyākṣaḥ sa dānavaḥ vipracittiḥ ca danujaḥ prahlādaḥ ca mahā balaḥ ūcire dānavān sarvān daityān ca bhaya vihvalān bho bho dānava śārdūlāḥ daityāḥ ca sura śatravaḥ kim pālayatha tiṣṭhadhvam mā bhayam vaḥ astu dānavāḥ kva gatāḥ dānavāḥ yūyam sukham lapsyatha vā na vā ete balam a jānantaḥ devāḥ pūrvam mahā balāḥ niḥśeṣān pramathiṣyanti pātāle a api kula adhamāḥ mā yāta vinivartadhvam rirakṣiṣata sarvaśas kva ca vairam suraiḥ sārdham kva rājya haraṇam gatam yat ca vaḥ garjitam pūrvam tasya kālaḥ āgataḥ vayam ekaikaśas yogyāḥ devānām na aśane ca nivartadhvam adharma jñāḥ yudhyadhvam kṛta niścayāḥ kaśyapasya sutaḥ śrīmān hiraṇyākṣaḥ a asmi dānavāḥ hiraṇyakaśipoḥ vīryam yat abhūt asura uttamāḥ dviguṇam tat mama api asti vīkṣadhvam tat aśaṅkitāḥ adya ca etān mayā sarvān hatān paśyata dānavāḥ saṃgrāme yadi tiṣṭhanti sarve devāḥ mama agratas vijvarāḥ mokṣyatha sukham mayā indreṇa asura uttamāḥ sa putram mām yudhi yathā vīkṣamāṇāḥ tu tiṣṭhata vinivṛttāḥ punar sarve lajjayā ati samāvṛtāḥ te chatra saṃchādita vaktra padmāḥ lajjā samāviṣṭa virāga vaktrāḥ atha te dānavāḥ vyāsa nivṛttāḥ raṇa mūrdhani duṣkaram tam tadā cakruḥ vegam nadyaḥ iva arṇave vipracittiḥ ca ṣaṇḍaḥ ca hradaḥ ca sa śatodaraḥ yamaḥ aasuraḥ ca vyaṃsaḥ ca śatāvarttaḥ huta udaraḥ tāraḥ ca tārakaḥ ca eva bakaḥ ca aṭiḥ ca viśrutaḥ ketuḥ ca śataketuḥ ca rāhuḥ svarbhānuḥ eva ca kālanemī vipākaḥ ca pākaḥ duryodhanaḥ tathā vṛṣaparvā śatākrandaḥ śatāvarttaḥ aatha dhenukaḥ saiṃhikeyāḥ ca vikhyātāḥ paulomāḥ kālakā sutāḥ nivāta kavacāḥ ca eva bāleyāḥ ca tathā gaṇāḥ bāṇasya ca sutāḥ sarve kālakhañjāḥ ca viśrutāḥ vṛtraḥ vṛttaḥ ca sa gaṇaḥ tathā anye su mahā balāḥ vegaḥ sa sindhurājasya velām prati yathā mahān vāditra rava saṃmiśraiḥ siṃhanādaiḥ su puṣkalaiḥ ratha nemi hayānām ca gajānām bṛṃhitaiḥ saha devāḥ hi api su saṃyattāḥ sarva udyogena saṃsthitāḥ pratyagṛhṇanta tān daityān krodha tāmra āyata īkṣaṇāḥ asibhiḥ pātyamānaiḥ ca śataghnībhiḥ ca sarvatas śara asi bhinna kṛttāni patanti urvyām śirāṃsi hi mukuṭaiḥ hā keyūraiḥ śroṇī sūtraiḥ ca saprabhaiḥ niṣkaiḥ dukūlaiḥ kaṭakaiḥ aṅgulīyaiḥ sa kuṇḍalaiḥ uracchadaiḥ kaṅkatībhiḥ karapālaiḥ ca dantinām paṭṭaiḥ ca kāñcanaiḥ divyaiḥ kavacaiḥ ca mahā prabhaiḥ tatra yuddha niśā bhāti tārābhiḥ dyauḥ iva aparā tiṣṭha tiṣṭha kva me yāsi na asti mokṣaḥ tava adya vai kim garjasi vṛthā vyomni śaradi iva balāhakāḥ karmaṇā darśaya kṣipram garjitam te aadya niṣphalam na evam prahriyate mūḍha mā vīkṣasva dṛḍhaḥ bhava adya te ratām sarvām deva adhama su duḥsahām nāśayiṣyanti mad bāṇāḥ tiṣṭha tiṣṭha mama agratas evam vadantaḥ yuyudhuḥ yathā mattāḥ mahā gajāḥ siddhāḥ ca cāraṇāḥ ca eva vyomni sarve vyavasthitāḥ mama ayam dānavaḥ saṃkhye hataḥ bhartā vinirmitaḥ ayam devaḥ tava patiḥ mā pārakye manaḥ kṛthāḥ kim evam lobha yuktā asi śreṣṭham śreṣṭham parīkṣase asaṃtuṣṭam manaḥ kim te saṃtuṣṭā bhava sundari bhāgaḥ a ayam te suraiḥ dattaḥ tam gṛhītvā vraja ālayam mekhalā valaya unmiśram tāsām tat kala bhāṣitam śrutvā śrutvā ca te yodhāḥ yuyudhuḥ bhaya varjitāḥ valgita āsphoṭitaiḥ ca eva diśaḥ sarvāḥ vyanādayan saha devaiḥ surādhyakṣaḥ kuśāḥ tasya śarāḥ matāḥ āyudhāni vicitrāṇi sruvāḥ yaiḥ hūyate haviḥ haviḥ prāṇāḥ samākhyātāḥ agniḥ mṛtyuḥ mahā prabhā mantrāḥ ca astrāṇi divyāni puroḍāśāḥ śirāṃsi api ādityāḥ bahvṛcaḥ ca āsan hotram ca vasubhiḥ kṛtam śāmitram ca aśvinau tau tu yajñe evam vyavarttata evam babhau tadā yajñaḥ daityānām śiśirādhipaḥ hiraṇyākṣaḥ abhavat yajvā daitya dānava nāyakaḥ vipracittiḥ abhūt hotā prasthātāraḥ ca dānavāḥ udgātā ca eva prahlādaḥ śukraḥ brahmā ca suvrataḥ paśuḥ indraḥ sahasra akṣaḥ evam saḥ aayajat avyayaḥ tasmin tadā mahā yajñe vartamāne mahā bhaye vāyuḥ abhyāyayau tūrṇam vipracittim mahā balaḥ saktaḥ saṃvareṇa aṃśaḥ balena bhagaḥ eva ca bāṇena varuṇaḥ sārdham andhakena yamaḥ saha ahirbudhnyena rāhuḥ ca kāpālī śataketunā hiraṇyākṣaḥ tadā indrasya dṛḍham āyamya kārmukam ṣaḍbhiḥ lalāṭe vivyādha gajam bhūyas ca saptabhiḥ gaurikā ambu parisrāvī śvetaḥ parvatarāj iva prahāram adadat vyāsa gaṇḍa veśe nanāda ca a cintayānaḥ sa gajam śakram eva ardayat balī sa vegam mahat āsthāya aṅkuśena ca coditaḥ hiraṇyākṣa ratham tūrṇam jagrāha ratha kūbare gṛhītvā sa tam utkṣipya cikṣepa gaja sattamaḥ meru śṛṅgam iva utkṣipya sa megham śveta kuṇḍalī svarbhānunā hataḥ pūrvam sa rathaḥ bhāskaraḥ yathā tatra samyak sthitam bhūmau hiraṇyākṣa ratham punar ājagāma gajaḥ tūrṇam jighṛkṣuḥ muni sattama tam āpatantam vegena samīkṣya asura sattamaḥ tam tu maṇḍalam āvṛttam gajaḥ tathā eva hi stabdha dṛś karṇa lāṅgūlaḥ prasārita karaḥ hāhākṛtam abhūt sarvam sādhu sādhu iti ca ubhayoḥ balayoḥ deva daityānām mūka vat ca abhavat punar sādhu daitya iti ca anyeṣām hiraṇyākṣa iti ca eva hi hiraṇyākṣaḥ ca daitya indraḥ bhramitvā tris mahā asuraḥ sa taiḥ śaraiḥ tadā vyāsa marma saṃdhiṣu tāḍitaḥ vajreṇa śatadhā tasya kavacam pravyaśātayat hayānām ca eva yoktrāṇi sarvāṇi eva tā akṛntata raśmīn ca sūta hasta sthān pratodam ca eva kāñcanam hiraṇyākṣaḥ gadayā samatāḍayat śakṛt mūtram sṛjan vyāsa prādravat balavān tadā asinā abhyāhataḥ ca api devarājaḥ mahā balaḥ amṛṣyamāṇaḥ prayayau pramāthinā hiraṇyanetreṇa yudhi pramāthitaḥ vipracittiḥ mahā māyaḥ vāyum bahubhiḥ āśugaiḥ iṣūn cikṣepa tīkṣṇa agrān śalabhān sāgaraḥ yathā dhārāḥ balāhakāt yadvat jyotsnāḥ candramasaḥ yathā tathā te śara saṃghātāḥ dhanuṣaḥ maṇḍalīkṛtāt bhāskaram chādayānān tān śarān āpatataḥ balī chinnāḥ ca niṣṭananti urvyām niraya sthāḥ yathā narāḥ tān tathā nihatān dṛṣṭvā vipracittiḥ mahā asuraḥ amanyanta surāḥ ca eva nigṛhītam danoḥ sutam athā utpatya tadā vegāt gadayā pavanam dṛḍham atāḍayat hiraṇyākṣaḥ gaṇḍa deśe mahā balaḥ sa tena su prahāreṇa hiraṇyākṣasya parityajya yayau tūrṇam anya deśam mahā balaḥ aṃśaḥ aasya āpatataḥ tūrṇam saṃvarasya mahā balaḥ dhanuḥ ca pañcadhā kṛtvā hayān ninye yama kṣayam bhūyas enam catuḥṣaṣṭyā sāyakānām avidhyata sa māyām tāmasīm ghorām samāviśya āśu dānavaḥ kaṃkaṭaiḥ karavālaiḥ ca bhindipālaiḥ ayaḥ ghanaiḥ jaghāna sa ratham devam aṃśam vyāsa raṇa ajire sa saṃdṛśya samantāt ca pātyamānam mahā bhayam aṃśaḥ mūḍhaḥ raṇam tyaktvā prayayau anyatas bhayāt bhagaḥ balam samāsādya rathena ambuda nādinā tiṣṭha tiṣṭha iti tam daityam uktvā vivyādha patriṇā sa sāyakam samāsādya balaḥ a asura mahā rathaḥ tasya tat lāghavam śauryam amūḍha tvam sa vīrya tām bhagaḥ lajjayā āviṣṭaḥ padā tena āhataḥ tadā nāma śeṣān tadā kṛtvā vinanāda yathā ambudaḥ syandanam tu balaḥ tyaktvā cūrṇitam sa hayam tadā bhagasya kārmukam divyam bhūmi sthaḥ ca ācchinat balī tatas enam pṛṣatkena lalāṭe samatāḍayat gṛhṇataḥ kārmukam ca asya pṛṣṭha deśe balaḥ aacchinat śaraiḥ viṣṭambhayāmāsa nandī mṛtyum yathā ātmanaḥ atha pratihatam matvā muśalam saḥ a asuraḥ punar dhanuṣkoṭyā bhagam mūrdhni balavān samatāḍayat ratham ca saha sūtena gṛhītvā kūbare punar cikṣepa dakṣiṇasyām sa vāyuḥ girim iva arṇave pūṣā virocanam devaḥ śaraiḥ bahubhiḥ āśugaiḥ kruddhaḥ pūṣā tathā ākṛṣya balavān kārmukam mahat tam āpatantam bahudhā nadantam diśaḥ ca sarvāḥ avabhāsayantam sa ca api avaplutya mahā asura indraḥ dṛṣṭvā tam āditya samāna bhāsam kṛtvā āśu bhasma iva yathā hutāśaḥ jagāma kham divyam adṛśya rūpam vegena asim viniṣkṛṣya sa pūṣṇaḥ niśitān śarān ciccheda āpatataḥ sarvān kārmukam ca eva vīryavān patitam tam sa daitya indraḥ pragṛhya āśu mahā balaḥ cikṣepa devarājānam nardamānaḥ payoda vat tam sārathiḥ mṛtyu bhayāt viṣaṇṇam samupasthitam apovāha tadā vyāsa tatas daityāḥ vicukruśuḥ bāṇam vivyādha varuṇaḥ pṛṣatkānām śatena ha sa kārmuka śatāni sma pañca bāṇaḥ pratāpavān ākṛṣya yugapad kruddhaḥ śarān cikṣepa tāvataḥ chinnām dṛṣṭvā ca tām bhūyas gadāḥ pañca asṛjat balī saḥ ekasyām tadā saktaḥ tāḥ gadāḥ na abhyavaikṣata avikṣatāḥ ca tāḥ sarvāḥ petuḥ tasya tatas upari kācid ca kārmukam ghoram hasta deśāt nyapātayat kācid dhvajam dṛḍham divyam cikṣepa dharaṇī tale kācid ratham tadā āhatya pāṃsu grāsam cakāra ha urasi ūru udare kaṭyām aparāḥ tasya tāḥ gadāḥ patantyaḥ su mahā ghorāḥ ārtim cakruḥ mahā bhayāḥ evam sa tena virathaḥ kṛtaḥ kṛcchreṇa dhīmatā varuṇaḥ tam samutsṛjya jagāma anyat raṇa ajiram yamaḥ aandhakam samāsādya tiṣṭha tiṣṭha iti coditaḥ andhakaḥ ratham tyaktvā gadā pāṇiḥ mahā balaḥ daṇḍena eva gadām ugrām bhasma sāyujyam ānayat mayā vairam samāsādya kva ante jīvam vimokṣyase agre draṣṭāsi tatra eva sarvān etān suradviṣaḥ uvāca tam yamam kruddham krodhāt dīpta anala īkṣaṇaḥ hiraṇyākṣa sutaḥ dṛptaḥ sura antakaḥ mahā yoga bala upetaḥ na asmi prākṛta dānavaḥ aṅga ādyena samutkṛtya bhāgān māṃsasya kārṣikān saṃvatsara śatāni aṣṭau pañca ca eva hutaḥ tuṣṭaḥ brahmā dadau yasya sadā eva amara tām śubhām saḥ a aham nāmnā mahā sattvaḥ mṛtyu utsedhaḥ na saṃśayaḥ na tvam mama kṛtaḥ mṛtyuḥ siṃhasya iva vane śaśaḥ aham tu bhavitā mṛtyuḥ tava na asti atra saṃśayaḥ na tvayā āsāditam pūrvam haratā jīvitāni hi kiṃcid bhūtam mahat bhūtam tena tvam nirbhayaḥ sadā prāṇaiḥ saha eva dehānte nāśayiṣyāmi sarvaśas daṇḍena asura śārdūlam uttamāṅge aabhyatāḍayat kālaḥ ca tam kareṇa eva hṛdaye samatāḍayat mṛtyuḥ ca aśaninā kṣipram śirasi eva abhyatāḍayat kaṇṭhe ca niyatiḥ tasya pāśam cikṣepa dāruṇam kṛtāntaḥ parighena eva gaṇḍa deśe samāhanat puṣpa vṛṣṭyā yathā kaścid ārtimān abhavat tathā na pāśāḥ tasya līyante daṇḍaḥ vā parighaḥ vā anyāni ca asya tīvrāṇi na duḥkhāya bhavanti uta tatas sarvaiḥ tadā utkruṣṭam dānavaiḥ sarvatodiśam avādyanta ca tūryāṇi mumude ca andhakaḥ bhṛśam sa teṣām tuṣṭi śabdena mada vīrya samīritaḥ ājaghāna mahā vegaḥ muṣṭinā urasi tam yamam hayānām ca eva yoktrāṇi pratodam ca eva kāñcanam avidhyetām tadā yasya kārmuke sāyakaiḥ śitaiḥ atha śakrasutaḥ śrīmā labdhvā tasya tadā antaram atha yāvat sa nistriṃśam jagrāha aditinandanaḥ tāvat enam padā daityaḥ vāmena urasi atāḍayat tatas hāhākṛtam abhūt asurāṇām bale mahat tūryāṇi ca api avādyanta sādhu sādhu iti ca abhavat jayantaḥ yathā cakram vidhātā balavān tadā kālakeyāḥ ca te daityāḥ paulomāḥ siṃhikā sutāḥ hiraṇyākṣa pathe kṣiptvā prādravat yena devatāḥ tasya asyataḥ tatas bāṇān candramāḥ prahasan iva sa tasya hasataḥ śīghram lalāṭe kaṅka patriṇā cakrarakṣān ca nistriṃśaiḥ anayat yama sādanam māyām divyām samāsthāya rāhuḥ samabhavat tadā sa rāhu bhūtaḥ daitya indraḥ deveśam śaśalakṣaṇam sa tām māyām tadā jñātvā devaḥ hi amṛta sambhavaḥ saḥ daityaḥ mahā māyaḥ tān śarān māyayā eva tu vārayan abhidudrāva śītaraśmim mahā asuraḥ gṛhītvā kūbare daityaḥ cikṣepa ca rarāsa ca dhanuḥ dhvajam hayān ca eva samacūrṇayat avyayaḥ aśanim śata ghaṇṭām sa dīptām mṛtyoḥ iva snuṣām jagrāha bhṛśam āyastaḥ pāśam mṛtyoḥ iva antakaḥ bhrāmayitvā sa tām ugrām yama daṇḍa nibhām balāt cikṣepa rāhum uddiśya vidyutam megharāj iva papāta rāhoḥ śirasi yathā vajram śiloccaye sa prahasya bhṛśam rāhuḥ vegam cakre mahā balaḥ ahirbudhnam prati kruddhāḥ nāgānām iva pakṣirāj śataketuḥ ca tam vīraḥ rathena ambu nādinā ādhāvataḥ kāpālī hṛdayam kaṅka patribhiḥ tatas hāhākṛtam daityaiḥ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa iti ca api uta śambaraḥ ca ilvalaḥ ca eva bāṇaḥ baliḥ atha api ca kālakeyāḥ sa paulomāḥ nivāta kavacāḥ ca ye te śaraiḥ ca gadābhiḥ ca muśalaiḥ prāśa tomaraiḥ kālanemiḥ tu saṃkruddhaḥ gadām hema pariṣkṛtām śata ghaṇṭā aṭṭahāsām ca mṛtyoḥ jihvām iva adbhutām bhrāmayitvā asakṛt daityaḥ nardamānaḥ yathā vṛṣaḥ śakra muktām iva āyāntīm aśanim parvata upari vidyutam vā mahā śaktām sa rudraḥ bhīma vikramaḥ vañcayāmāsa vegena śvetaḥ mṛtyum iva āgatām tiṣṭha tiṣṭha iti ca api uktvā rudraḥ praharatām varaḥ bhrāmayitvā gadām ghorām daityasya upari apātayat sa tayā gadayā daityaḥ dīptayā bhīma rūpayā hataḥ mūrdhni diśaḥ sarvāḥ hāridrāḥ samapaśyata aja eka pādam gadayā skandha deśe vyatāḍayat saṃstabhya gadayā bhūyas rudram kukṣau atāḍayat sa tayā gadayā nunnaḥ yathā vāyu īritaḥ nagaḥ papāta bhūmau vegena śataśṛṅgaḥ yathā purā bhrāmayitvā ca cikṣepa sa ca amṛtyuḥ avasthitaḥ kārtasvanam jvaraḥ ca ugraḥ rudraḥ praharatām varaḥ kārtasvanaḥ ca bāṇena kārmukam tasya dīptimat rudraḥ daṇḍam sarva artha sādhakam tam yuga anta anala prakhyam śata sūrya anala prabham hanyamānaḥ ca taiḥ daṇḍaḥ haviṣā iddhaḥ iva analaḥ tam a pratihatam daṇḍam jñātvā kārtasvanaḥ sa tu daṇḍaḥ ca ratham abhyetya sa hayam saha yantṛṇā chittvā dhvajam dhanuḥ ca asya tathā yoktrāṇi vājinām viratham tam jvaram kṛtvā asinā mūrdhni atāḍayat asi ghāta parīta ātmā sa rudraḥ śatru saṃsadam aryamā bahubhiḥ bāṇaiḥ prahlādam daitya puṅgavam ājaghāna ratham ghoram dhvajam ca asya vyaśātayat jaghāna bahubhiḥ bāṇaiḥ vṛṣṭyā meghaḥ yathā acalam śarān a gaṇayan eva hiraṇyakaśipoḥ sutaḥ nirjitāḥ tarjitāḥ ca eva yuddhāya ca punar sthitāḥ paraspara jigīṣūṇām yuddham bhīru bhaya āvaham tatas bheryaḥ ca śaṅkhāḥ ca paṭaha āsekya puṣkalāḥ jharjharyaḥ pepukāḥ ca eva ḍiḍṇimāḥ goviṣāṇakāḥ avādyanta bale vyāse ubhayeṣām sahasraśas keśa śaivāla bahulā dhanuḥ jyā kṛta tantukā niṣṭanat śastra maṇḍūkā dukūla chatra phenilā uṣṇīṣa vara haṃsā ca kara pāda śira upalā hāra keyūra sikatā mukuṭa utphulla paṅkajā medaḥ usra kapha paṅkā ca prāṇa jīvita nāśinī tasyām snāyanti rakṣāṃsi pibanti ca ramanti ca nṛtyanti ca api bhaya dāḥ tathā gṛdhrāḥ gomāyavaḥ ca eva śvānaḥ mārjāra vāyasāḥ cakṣūṃṣi kara pāda antram bhakṣayantaḥ mama ayam hastinaḥ pādaḥ mama ayam meda paṭṭakaḥ mama ayam ca karaḥ śukraḥ bāhuḥ ca rudhirasya daḥ mama ayam tava na ayam ca gṛhṇa paśya iti ca eva hi kalaham cakrire tatra krūrāḥ piśita bhojanāḥ tyaktvā anye ca hatān tatra sva dehān su mahā rathāḥ vimānaiḥ strī jana ākīrṇaiḥ prayayuḥ brahmaṇaḥ sadaḥ apare pāpa karmāṇaḥ hanyamānāḥ ruṣā andhitaiḥ kecid ārtta svaram kṛtvā ṣa arditāḥ mriyante puruṣāḥ yuddhe śataśas aatha sahasraśas daśantaḥ ca tṛṇāni anye bhrātar putra iti ca apare krandamānāḥ janāḥ bhītāḥ prāṇān kṛcchreṇa tatyajuḥ śara sannata marmāṇaḥ patanti urvyām drumāḥ iva aśivāni śivāḥ neduḥ aṭṭahāsān ca rākṣasāḥ ārtta nādān ca puruṣāḥ tathā eva gaja vājinaḥ siṃhanādāḥ ca śūrāṇām bāhu śabdāḥ ca puṣkalāḥ dhanuṣām kṛṣyamāṇānām śabdāḥ vādya ravāḥ tathā varuṇaḥ marutaḥ ca eva viśvedevāḥ ca sarvaśas mitraḥ ca eva jayantaḥ ca bhagaḥ pūṣā aryamā tathā kāpālī ca api yaḥ rudraḥ vasavaḥ ca mahā balāḥ ete ca anye ca devānām pravarāḥ bāhu śālinaḥ prabhañjanāḥ iva akāle mahāntaḥ iva ca acalāḥ grasantaḥ ca yathā grāhāḥ chindantaḥ makarāḥ iva varṣamāṇāḥ iva ghanāḥ siṃḍāḥ iva ca nardinaḥ dānavānām balam sarve vegena eva abhyavārayan teṣām tad vega nunnānām bhagnānām kliśyatām punar trāṇa artham ati dīpta akṣaḥ hiraṇyākṣaḥ aagrahīt padam andhakaḥ ca mahā māyaḥ bhrātaraḥ ca asya tad samāḥ śatam ratha sahasrāṇām sarveṣām apalāyinām grāhāṇām iva kāṇḍe ca gajānām iva vā vane indraḥ tvaṣṭā ca pūṣā ca hiraṇyākṣam avārayan saṃgatya sāyakaiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ sarva marmasu atāḍayan teṣām dhanūṃṣi citrāṇi asyatām ca divaukasām tāni viṣṭambhitāni sma tadā daityena māyayā nānamanti yathā pūrvam tatas te hi ākulāḥ abhavan babandha teṣām gātrāṇi samantāt astra māyayā tān baddhān ca samīkṣya evam akarmaṇyān mahā balān yantāraḥ vāhanaiḥ kṣipram jahnu stambhāt raṇa ajirāt pragṛhīta āyudhāḥ kruddhāḥ tiṣṭha tiṣṭha iti vādinaḥ teṣām āpatatām saṃkhye sa daityaḥ krodha mūrcchitaḥ sarveṣām sāyakaiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ bibheda kavacān yudhi yamaḥ daṇḍam sasarja asya tathā kālaḥ ca dhaneśaḥ ca aśanim ghoram varuṇaḥ pāśam eva ca bhagaḥ ca muśalam ghoram anye ca anyān tathā amarāḥ sādhuvādān tatas hṛṣṭāḥ dānavānām mahā rathāḥ samam cakruḥ ca sampūjya hiraṇyākṣam mahā asuram kaśyapena prasūtasya rājñaḥ a asmākam ca svāminaḥ cakruḥ hṛṣṭāḥ ca vādyāni tathā sarvāṇi avādayan evam hi samprahṛṣṭānām dānavānām mahā āhave pārāśarya giraḥ bahvyaḥ śrūyante teṣām praharṣam jñātvā ittham deva bhaṅgam ca tam tadā samantāt dhvaja yuktena patākā śata mālinā javena yantR̥ṇām yatnāt nigṛhītena śabdavān prapūrayan diśaḥ sarvāḥ sahasā pratyadṛśyata sa pañcabhiḥ śaraiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ daitya rājam atha avyayaḥ āhatya yojane tasmāt deśāt kṣipram avāsṛjat andhakam ca tatas ṣaṣṭyā vipracittim ca saptabhiḥ mayam pañcāśatā viddhvā saptabhiḥ saptabhiḥ punar punar puloma putrān ca nivāta kavacān api te tena bahubhiḥ viddhāḥ śaraiḥ asura puṅgavāḥ dikṣu sarvāsu vikṣiptāḥ yojana antaritāḥ sthitāḥ yān yān ca te vimuñcanti gadā muśala paṭṭiśān tān sarvān bhagavān vāyuḥ dānava upari apātayat te hatāḥ hanyamānāḥ ca sva muktaiḥ āyudhaiḥ bhṛśam pāṃśu varṣeṇa ca punar cakṣūṃṣi eṣām apūrayat śastrāṇi ca tathā anyāni puñjaśas ca avatsthire dhik vaḥ daityāḥ balam sarvam dhik darpāḥ dhik parākramāḥ ye yūyam raṇa madhya sthāḥ jīvite sati bāliśāḥ athavā yat mayā proktam yūyam bālāḥ rati klamāḥ tat tathā nā asti saṃdehaḥ bālaiḥ iva hi vaḥ kṛtam tiṣṭhadhvam bāla vat sarve rakṣantaḥ jīvitam svakam tatas sa parvataḥ bhūtvā diti putraḥ pratāpavān samantāt vāyu vegam tam samavārayat avyayaḥ ro anilam jñātvā parvatena nivāritam sa baddhaḥ bāhu pāśena tadā vāyuḥ pratāpavān sampragṛhya andhakam śīghram utpapāta vihāyasā rāhuḥ gadām mahā raudrām kṣipram mūrdhni nyapātayat jagrāha su mahā grāhaḥ matsyān iva mahā arṇave sa tena su prahāreṇa vihvalaḥ prāṇi gocaraḥ alātam iva saṃdīptam niśāyām muni sattama vegam cakruḥ mahā vegāḥ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa iti vādinaḥ ālakṣya su bahūn saṃkhye garjamānaḥ payoda vat abhīkṣṇam bhīma saṃhrādam vāyum dānava yūthapaḥ sa ca api tena vikṣiptaḥ labdha saṃjñaḥ mahā balaḥ tatas vāyuḥ ca devāḥ ca sarve eva saha anugāḥ siṃhānām iva dṛptānām vijane śarabhaiḥ saha tatra kecid vinihatāḥ su prahāreṇa mūrcchitāḥ sādhu sādhu iti bhāṣantaḥ prāṇān ūrdhvam vitatyajuḥ apare danu daitya indrāḥ mudā yuktāḥ mahā balāḥ pareṇa abhyāhatam śūram samarakṣan su putra vat a vikṣatāḥ śitaiḥ śastraiḥ ātmānam garhayanti uta aśastram kecid abhyetya deva daityāḥ mahā balāḥ devāḥ ca na āhatāḥ ye vai na abhighnanti kadācana sādinaḥ sādibhiḥ sārdham rathinaḥ rathibhiḥ saha evam teṣām hi saṃgrāmaḥ devānām dānavaiḥ saha āvartata śatāni aṣṭau varṣāṇi su mahā bhayaḥ jatru deśe śaram ghoram nicakhāna rarāsa ca anubhūya prahāram tam sa daityaḥ bhīma vikramaḥ tomareṇa bibheda āśu kumbha deśe mahā balaḥ anaṅkuśaḥ svam balam hi anivāritaḥ bhaya arditāḥ diśaḥ sarvāḥ palāyante sma sarvaśas siṃhanādān ca saṃcakruḥ daitya dānava saṃmatāḥ tat deva sainyam saṃdṛśya daitya dānava yūthapaiḥ vadhyamānam bhṛśam vyāsa vyādhaiḥ iva mṛgān vane tigma varṣmā su śīta arciḥ svayam pavana sārathiḥ somaḥ tu daśabhiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ sāyakaiḥ marma bhedibhiḥ moham samāviśat vyāsa krauñcaḥ śattyā eva parvataḥ tam tathā abhisamīkṣya eva ratham yantā saha asuram apovāha mahā māyam dhaneśam nidhayaḥ yathā tatas hāhākṛtam daityaiḥ daivataiḥ sādhu sādhu iti gṛhṇa gṛhṇa iti daityaiḥ ca mā gṛhṇa iti ca daivataiḥ avādyanta surāṇām vai harṣa rāga pravartanāḥ atha andhakaḥ ca tāraḥ ca tārakaḥ namuciḥ tathā vipracittiḥ śaṃvaraḥ ca mahā māyaḥ ca dānavaḥ dhundhuḥ kārtasvanaḥ ca eva rāhuḥ ca eva mahā balaḥ śataketuḥ śatāvartaḥ pulomā vilvalaḥ tathā vātāpī kālanemiḥ ca śatāvartaḥ śatodaraḥ mahāpārśva udaraḥ ca eva musuṇḍaḥ suṇḍaḥ eva ca vṛṣaparvā atikāyaḥ ca śarabhaḥ siṃhaḥ eva ca virocanaḥ baliḥ bāṇaḥ vātāśvaḥ ca mahā balaḥ dundubhiḥ mahiṣaḥ ca eva ariṣṭaḥ riṣṭaḥ eva ca sūcīlomā tuhuṇḍaḥ ca huṇḍaḥ ca paratāpanaḥ aśvagrīvaḥ sambaraḥ ca tathā anyaḥ kālasaṃvaraḥ śālvaḥ śatabhujaḥ ca eva drumaḥ vyāghraḥ ca dānavaḥ chāyāgrahaḥ ca vikhyātaḥ saiṃhikeyāḥ ca viśrutāḥ hālāhalāḥ ca te sarve bali putrāḥ ca viśrutāḥ paulomāḥ kālakeyāḥ ca vipracitti sutāḥ ca ye mahiṣāḥ iva hastinaḥ śarabham yathā vighnāḥ yadvat kāmāḥ mokṣa āśritam yathā indriya arthāḥ yathā yogam anarthāḥ duḥkhitam yathā nāgāḥ iva garutmānam bhramarāḥ iva pādapam dudruvuḥ śiśiram devam mahā jalada nisvanāḥ teṣām āpatatām vyāsa timirāriḥ hasan iva udyatāni āyudhāni sma śaraiḥ ciccheda sarvaśas tatas sa tān punar devaḥ sarvān eva ruṣā anvitaḥ hṛdayeṣu śaraiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ bibheda ca rarāsa ca tuṣāreṇa ca śītena tārādhipatiḥ avyayaḥ te śaraiḥ ca vinirbhinnāḥ śītena ca bhṛśa arditāḥ sthitāḥ tatra eva daitya indrāḥ citra paṭṭa arpitāḥ iva candraḥ a api devaḥ samare maṇḍalīkṛta kārmukaḥ nicakhāna śarān aṣṭau mukhe tasya api candramāḥ yathā sa bhagavān devaḥ candramāḥ dānava ardanaḥ hanyamāne mahā māyaḥ samāśvasya tadā asuraḥ hiraṇākṣaḥ diteḥ putraḥ idam āha ruṣā anvitaḥ garutmanaḥ sutam vīram ratha yantāram āhave khageśvara na te lajjā bhavati iha raṇa arṇave rājā asmākam jitaḥ saṃkhya śatrubhiḥ śatru tāpanaḥ jīvita eṣī parityajya bālān asmān durātmavān raṇāt apagataḥ kvāpi dhik mām aśubha kāriṇam kaśyapasya sutaḥ bhūtvā bhrātā daityasya saṃmataḥ rājā svāmī ca śūraḥ ca nirjitaḥ śatrubhiḥ katham tam eva pakṣam bhajase na mām tvam parirakṣase khaḍgena anena tīkṣṇena chettum khaga kula adhama atas kṛtvā abhirakṣāmi gaccha muktaḥ asi doṣavān mā krudhaḥ dānava śreṣṭha na asmi daitya tava ahitaḥ tadā upavāhitaḥ kṣipram raṇāt rakṣā artham eva hi sūtāḥ śāstra vidaḥ ca eva bruvate dānava īśvara rathinā śastra ghātebhyaḥ rakṣyaḥ yantā yathā priyā aśaktaḥ syāt iti iti evam mayā kṣipram apohitaḥ āśvasya balam ādhāya punar yuddhena daṃśitaḥ ripūn jeṣyasi saṃgrāme tu evam tvam apavāhitaḥ ye hi kāle sma kāle ca āyānti daṃśitāḥ teṣām na asulabhaḥ daitya jayaḥ yuddhe mahātmanām aham garutmanaḥ putraḥ devān hitvā asurān śritaḥ tava eva daitya snehena mā śaṅkām tvam kṛthāḥ mayi māyām atha samāsthāya dhanuṣā vā balena vā jahi śatrūn mahā rāja mā ruṣantu diteḥ sutāḥ śaraiḥ āśīviṣa ākāraiḥ dhvājam cicchidatuḥ yudhi tatas lalāṭe vidhvā enam tau devau śaśi pāvakau tayoḥ tat lāghavam dṛṣṭvā hiraṇyākṣaḥ pratāpavān āgneyena astra mukhyena śiśiram tat vyaśātayat tuṣāra varṣaṇam dagdhvā tatas māyayā punar sa rāhuṇā tadā tena grasyamānaḥ khageśvaraḥ dānavāḥ hi api te sarve naṣṭe tasmin himāśaye te samudra sama nāditāḥ daitya dānava mahā rathāḥ pravara sādinaḥ āśvasantaḥ yuddha kāṃkṣiṇaḥ daṃṣṭriṇaḥ iva mahā gajāḥ vana samāśritāḥ sa śaraiḥ dānava ratham sa sūtam saha vājinam anye ca dānava śreṣṭhāḥ kuśānum paryavārayan saḥ tadā teṣām dhanūṃṣi ca rathān tathā kavacāni vicitrāṇi śirāṃsi ca vasūttamaḥ dadāha vikṛta ākāraḥ nādayan sarvatas diśaḥ jihvāḥ dahan babhau vyāsa guhāyām dīpta vat sthitaḥ keṣāṃcid pradahan tāni dānavānām mukhāni saḥ paṅkajāni iva phullāni timirāriḥ mahā prabhaḥ keṣāṃcid tu gaja indrāṇām karān kāñcana bhūṣitān śarān śarāsanān ca eva tathā khaḍga paricchadān te hanyamānāḥ daitya indrāḥ viśastrāḥ duḥkhitāḥ bhṛśam teṣām vahni pradīptānām dahyatām sura vidviṣām hiraṇyākṣaḥ svayam rājā parjanyaḥ samapadyata tam praśāntam tadā ālakṣya vidhuram havyavāhanam vipracittiḥ śaraiḥ tīkṣṇaiḥ lalāṭe samavākirat mukhe śarān mahā ghorān nicakhāna mahā asuraḥ uttasthau vāyu vat dhūmaḥ śarīrāt añjana upamaḥ tena dhūmena kham ca eva divam bhūḥ ca eva sarvaśas tena śabdena saṃjñābhiḥ nāma viśrāvaṇaiḥ api āṭim daityāḥ prakurvanta vakaḥ iti eva devatāḥ ca surāḥ iti evam tat vyavartata evaṃbhūtam abhūt yuddham varṣāṇām tu sahasrakam jagat vināśāya jagat hita eṣiṇām sura asurāṇām rudhira ogha nisravam vāyunā abhihitaḥ ca eva agacchat vilayam bhṛśam su ghoṣakāḥ ḍiṇḍimāḥ ca bhagnāḥ bheryaḥ ca sarvaśas pūṣā bhagaḥ ca dvau etau aryamā mitraḥ eva ca tatra cāmīkara ābhāsam pragṛhya balavat dhanuḥ sa pañcabhiḥ śaraiḥ viddhvā hiraṇyākṣam mahā balam aśvān ca ekaikaśas sarvān jahāsa ca nanāda ca śarān āśīviṣa ākārān vyasṛjan vikṛta aśanān tam viśastram vikavacam kṛtvā te deva sattamāḥ yugapad śastra varṣeṇa tāḍayāmāsuḥ āhave yamaḥ daṇḍena tam mūrdhni śakraḥ vajreṇa ca ahanat vitta daḥ ca eva pāśena varuṇaḥ tathā saḥ daityaḥ tatas utpatya puṇḍasya āsthāya pṛṣṭhatas yamam muṣṭi prahāreṇa puṇḍāt bhūmau apātayat bhrāmayitvā akṣipat dūre naṣṭaḥ tvam iti ca abravīt tatas dhaneśasya punar śivikām kāma gāminīm sarve devāḥ diśaḥ sarvāḥ pranaṣṭāḥ jīvita arthinaḥ pūrveṇa bhagavān indraḥ saha ādityaḥ sa lokapaḥ dakṣiṇena tathā rudrāḥ vasavaḥ ca aśvinau api pratīcīm takṣa rakṣāṃsi piśācāḥ pannagaiḥ saha uttareṇa tathā anye ca prapadyanta yathā mṛgāḥ teṣu bhagneṣu daitya indrāḥ pṛṣṭhatas vikṛta āyudhāḥ tatas muhūrtam te gatvā hiraṇyākṣeṇa vāritāḥ nivṛttāḥ samatiṣṭhanta sarve aamarṣa vaśam gatāḥ athā uvāca tatas kruddhān hiraṇyākṣaḥ hasan iva daitya dānava nāthān tān idam vacana kovidaḥ kim ebhiḥ abalaiḥ adya yuṣmākam daitya dānavāḥ avabaddhaiḥ ca ruddhaiḥ ca mṛta saṃjñaiḥ durātmabhiḥ hatāḥ te na atra saṃdehaḥ yeṣām rājyam idam dhṛtam na eṣām jīvitam adya asti mā tvaradhvam su bāliśāḥ abalān api saṃrabdhān nirāśān jīvita eṣiṇaḥ avadhyā iti balāt ca eva durjayāḥ ca na saṃśayaḥ ca iti uvāca sadā uśanāḥ yat etat rājyam iti āhuḥ prāṇāḥ hi ete mahātmanām sa teṣām harati prāṇān yaḥ teṣām rājyam āharet teṣām adya suraiḥ āpūrayāma sthānāni devānām dānava īśvarāḥ tatas te sa hiraṇyākṣāḥ prāviśan amarāvatīm ratnāni jagṛhuḥ vyāsa vijayam ca avaghoṣayan ārakṣam āmanuḥ tatra cakṣuḥ āptaiḥ ca te a asurāḥ āśvāsayanta paurān ca tathā prakṛtayaḥ ca ha āśīrbhiḥ ca asura śreṣṭham hiraṇyākṣam praṇemire kāryāṇi kṛtvā sarveṣām agacchat varuṇālayam te siṃhanādaiḥ vividhaiḥ ca vādyaiḥ pravalgana āsphoṭita kūjitaiḥ ca mahā asurāḥ dānava rāja siṃham jagmuḥ samantāt parivārya sarve dṛṣṭvā varuṇa putrāḥ te pitrā saha vidudruvuḥ daityāḥ ca api hi te sarve prāpya tat varuṇālayam vyūḍha skandhāḥ vyatiṣṭhanta vyasṛjan tat puram prati dūtam vātāpinam ca eva rājñā preṣitam ūrjitam sa gatvā tām purīm ramyām praviśya ca mahā asuraḥ pauraiḥ su pūjitaḥ vyāsa prāha idam tān su garvitaḥ jitvā sarvān surān saṃkhye ājñāpayati vaḥ sadā yūyam śṛṇuta sarve me ājñām kuruta ca eva hi nārada ādyāḥ ca ṛṣayaḥ tam samāsādya daityakam ūcuḥ tam asuram vyāsa sāntva yuktam idam tadā sa tvam rājā ca bhojaḥ ca virāj samrāj tathā eva ca bhūpatiḥ kṣatriyaḥ ca eva nṛpaḥ sarva sahaḥ tathā kaḥ tvayā sadṛśaḥ loke bāhya ekam rudram īśvaram kaḥ tava ājñām mahā rāja pratihanyāt durātmavān vara daḥ tvam hi bhūtānām asmākam vara daḥ bhava ūcuḥ te daitya rājānam tadā vacana kovidāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ na tathā tat bhavet daityaḥ eṣ sa naḥ dīyatām varaḥ mama pratijñā vipra indrāḥ pūrvam eva tapodhanāḥ lokānām sukham edheyam rājā lokasya yadi aham mithyāpratijñaḥ syām ca aham asatyaḥ ca durātmavān kartāsmi tam tathā sarvam yāce a aham śirasā hi vaḥ hiraṇyākṣam idam vyāsa punar eva abruvan vibhum ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ pratijñā prabhavet sā yat lokānām suḥkha varjanam tat ca tasmāt na naśyeta brahmaṇaḥ kṛtiḥ avyayā evam tvayā anṛṇa tvam ca brahmaṇaḥ adya kṛtam bhavet ye drohāḥ śakra pāśāḥ ca mama ājñā bhaṅga kāriṇaḥ teṣām eva bhavet mṛtyuḥ narakeṣu ca yātanāḥ etat āha tadā rājā te ca api ūcuḥ narādhipam evam astu iti tān ca uktvā yātanā mokṣam ādiśat sa muktvā nārakān sarvān dadau sthānasya rakṣaṇam tatas sa jayam pūjayitvā tu tān dvijān te tām dhaneśasya purīm mahā balāḥ vinādayantaḥ a andhaka pālitāḥ tadā tathā laṅkām samāsādya dhaneśasya purīm śubhām adūre tasthuḥ uddhūtāḥ sannaddha kavaca āyudhāḥ laṅkā nivāsinaḥ sarve nidhi pālāḥ tathā eva ca argham ratnāni ca ādāya dānavāya nyavedayan ūcuḥ ca tava daitya indra vayam vaśyāḥ na saṃśayaḥ kiṃkarāḥ ca praśādhi asamān yathāvat dānava īśvara hiraṇyākṣaḥ uvāca mā bhīḥ bhavatu vaḥ yakṣāḥ mama yūyam na saṃśayaḥ prahlādaḥ deva saṃghānām sarveṣām viprabādhanaḥ anyeṣām deva saṃghānām sthānāni āpūrayat punar saṃsthāpya sarva sthāneṣu sthāninaḥ dānava īśvarān bhūrlokam ca akhilam daityaḥ svayam eva anvaśāsata bhūyas ca dhanuḥ ādāya sa śaram sūrya sannibhaḥ daitya indraḥ medinīm prāha mama ātmānam vidarśaya sā bhītā tasya daityasya vepamānā kṛtāñjaliḥ jaya iti uktvā agratas tasthau viṣṇoḥ lakṣmīḥ iva aparā tām mṛgīm iva śārdūlaḥ bhujaṅgīm iva pakṣirāj śārdūlīm siṃhaḥ iva ca siṃhīm śarabha rāj iva haṃsīm kākaḥ iva kṣudraḥ mayūrīm madgu rāj iva tathā tām sa diteḥ putraḥ jagrāha ruṣita ānanaḥ uvāca ca eva saṃrabdhaḥ tām devīm bhaya vihvalām tava aham vasudhe bhartā brahmaṇā prakṛtaḥ śubhe mā anyam patim tvam icchethāḥ rakṣikṣye a aham tatas hi te babaddha nāgapāśaiḥ tām duṣṭām iva yathā aṅganām ārtām rurodha duṣṭaḥ ca kandarām iva vīryavān kṛṣī bale iva kṣetram su niṣpannam samīkṣya ca saṃyujya sva sutam jyeṣṭham dāraiḥ sādhuḥ iva ātmavān yajadhvam dānavāḥ sarve viprān pūjayata iti ca devam ca śūlinam sarve namasyata punar punar na tasyāḥ dhārmikaḥ kaścid daityaḥ vā dānavaḥ oṣadhyaḥ svayam udbhūtāḥ tasmin rājani śāsati vinā kṛṣṭim sambhavanti sarva lokasya sādhikāḥ saḥ eva tāḥ samādāya kṣaram nayati daitya rāj saḥ eva daṇḍikaḥ abhūt ca daṇḍaḥ ca eva saḥ eva hi hiṃsakāḥ na ca tasya āsan na ca api adhārmikāḥ kvacid na santi tasya rakṣāṃsi na piśācāḥ ca hiṃsriṇaḥ na adhīyate yajante vai eka varṇam abhūt tadā bhṛgavaḥ ye abhavan viprāḥ dānavānām mudā āvahāḥ hṛṣṭa puṣṭam abhūt sarvam vyādhi duḥkha vivarjitam vyāsaḥ uvāca dharaṇyām ca eva baddhāyām sukhitāsu prajāsu ca sthānebhyaḥ cyāvitāḥ devāḥ hi apratiṣṭhāḥ mahā balāḥ kim akurvata duḥkha ārttāḥ etat me śaṃsa sarvaśas śṛṇuṣva dharma devānām avasthā yā babhūva ha daitya dānava bhāgānām vaktāraḥ śubha sādhakāḥ yajñe gate ca daitya indrāt devāḥ sarve mṛtāḥ iva abhavan naṣṭa saṃjñāḥ ca manda vīrya parākramāḥ niḥśrīkāḥ ca nirudyogāḥ nirākrandāḥ samutsukāḥ mantra dagdhāḥ yathā sarpāḥ vajra piṣṭāḥ yathā acalāḥ garutmatā abhisandaṣṭāḥ pannagāḥ iva nirviṣāḥ vihīna adbhiḥ yathā vṛkṣāḥ bāndhavaiḥ vā api yoṣitaḥ atha devaḥ svayambhūḥ tān jñātvā devān tathāvidhān bho devāḥ naṣṭa saṃjñāḥ stha kimartham duḥkhitāḥ bhṛśam yūyam lokasya kartāraḥ hartāraḥ ca eva sarvaśas svīyam yogam madīyam ca tyaktvā duḥkham mahā balāḥ na
