purandarāt sarva tejaḥ mayī devī mātR̥ṇām pravarā śubhā bahu māṃsā mahā vidyā babhūva vṛṣabhadhvajāt sva rūpa bala dhāriṇyaḥ nirjagmuḥ daitya nāśanāḥ vāyavyā vāruṇī yāmyā kauberī ca mahā balāḥ mahākālī tathā āgneyī anyāḥ ca eva sahasraśas tāḥ gatvā tat puram ramyam daityān bhīma parākramān daitya hīnam ca tat cakruḥ purā agryam hema bhūṣitam ājagāma pradeśam tam mātRṇām vara ditsayā tān dṛṣṭvā nihatān sarvān daityān amara vidviṣaḥ parituṣṭaḥ tadā tāsām varān prādāt vṛṣadhvajaḥ jagataḥ mātaraḥ yūyam mātṛ bhūtāḥ bhaviṣyathaḥ yuṣmākam ye bhaviṣyanti bhaktāḥ puruṣa puṅgavāḥ striyaḥ vā api mahābhāgāḥ na tān hiṃsanti hiṃsakāḥ mṛtāḥ mama gaṇāḥ ca api bhaviṣyanti ajara amarāḥ bhavatīnām idam sthānam koṭī varṣam iti śrutam aham hetuḥ hi yuṣmākam yasmāt sṛṣṭāḥ mayā eva ca hetukeśvara nāmnā aham sthāsyāmi atra vara pradaḥ yaḥ tu yuṣmān mayā sārdham vidhivat pūjayiṣyati dānavāḥ nihatāḥ yasmāt śūlena bahu māṃsayā śūlakuṇḍam idam nāmnā khyātam tīrtham bhaviṣyati iha śūla udakam pītvā bahu māṃsām praṇamya ca adhṛṣyaḥ sarva hiṃsrāṇām bhaviṣyati nara uttamaḥ pratikūlā iti vikhyātā ramyā mandākinī nadī mātṛtantrāṇi divyāni mātṛyajña vidhim param puṇyāni prakariṣyāmaḥ yajanam yaiḥ avāpsyatha brāhmam svāyambhuvam ca eva kaumāram yāmalam tathā sārasvatam sa gāndhāram aiśānam nandiyāmalam narāṇām yajamānānām varān yūyam pradāsyatha yāḥ ca nāryaḥ sadā yuṣmān yakṣyante sa rahasyataḥ yoga īśvaryaḥ bhaviṣyanti rāmāḥ divya parākramāḥ chagalaḥ kumbhakarṇaḥ ca madīyau gaṇa nāyakau yuṣmākam dvārapāla tve sthāsyataḥ tau mama ājñayā koṭī varṣam idam sthānam mātR̥ṇām priyam uttamam śmaśānam pravaram divyam bhaviṣyati sukha pradam varān evam hi tān labdhvā mātaraḥ loka mātaraḥ bhaktyā praṇamya deveśam mumuduḥ bhṛśam arditāḥ tatas prabhṛti tāḥ sarvāḥ sahitāḥ śaśimaulinā koṭī varṣe vasanti sma sarva loka abhaya pradāḥ abhavat śaṃkaraḥ vyāsa yat mām tvam pṛṣṭavān asi utpattim etām jagatī īśvarīṇām brahma indra śakra anila vanditānām paṭhet manuṣyaḥ śṛṇuyāt ca nityam yaḥ svarga lokam sa mṛtaḥ labheta śakreṇa girayaḥ pūrvam chinna pakṣāḥ yathā kṛtāḥ purā siṃha vapuḥ kṛtvā viṣṇunā parama ojasā hate pitari sa śrīmān mahā daitya pati īśvaraḥ bahūni abda sahasrāṇi cakāra ugram mahā tapaḥ praṇamya uvāca vara dam idam brahmāṇam āgatam rājānam hatavān viṣṇuḥ pitaram mama māyayā saṃgrāme tam aham viṣṇum yathā hanyām tathā kuru prasādāt devadevasya jagatsraṣṭuḥ umāpateḥ na eṣa śakyaḥ mayā dātum varaḥ tubhyam mahā asura anyam vṛṇu yathā iṣṭam tvam varam yāvat dadāmi te tasya viṣṇoḥ tatas deva śakrasya anyasya vā ripoḥ katham hanyām aham viṣṇum śakram ca eva śatakratum sarva śaila adhipaḥ śrīmān cakramat himavān giriḥ dvābhyām utpatya pakṣābhyām ājagāma viyat caraḥ purā hi girayaḥ vyāsa āsan pakṣa samanvitāḥ prāha idam vacanam prītaḥ kṛtvā svāgatam āditas devatāḥ bhrātaraḥ bhūtvā asmākam devatāḥ ca hi avamanya asurān asmān rājyam kurvanti darpitāḥ vayam yūyam ca saṃgatya jitvā devān aśeṣatas devānām yajanam yeṣu hūyante yeṣu ca agnayaḥ prīyante devatāḥ yeṣu deśān tān acala īśvara ijyā hīnān tatas devān sukham haṃsyāma nirvṛtāḥ na etat yuktam iti prāha sa āśaṅkaḥ dānava adhipam eṣa tvām sahitaḥ śailaiḥ neṣyāmi nidhanam samam daitya rājam tatas kruddham dṛṣṭvā parvata sattamaḥ atha śailaiḥ tadā sārdham himavān acala uttamaḥ pitṛ deva ātmikāḥ sarvāḥ yajña homa ādikāḥ kriyāḥ śakrāya aśrāvayat sarvam parvatānām vyatikramam imam vyatikramam prāptāḥ na doṣaḥ naḥ kathaṃcana tena idam kāritam sarvam yajña deśa upamardanam tān evam bruvataḥ śakraḥ sutarām manyumān girīn pakṣa hīnān tadā cakre kṣiptvā vajram punar punar hīna pakṣāḥ tatas te tu sarve eva acala īśvarāḥ petuḥ mahī tale vyāsa chinna pakṣāḥ khagāḥ iva pakṣa chedam naga indrāṇām bhṛśam cukrodha pīḍitaḥ kim karomi iti saṃcintya ciram daitya gaṇa adhipaḥ badaryāśramam āgatya dadarśa viṣṇum āsthitam tasya āsīt su mahat yuddham viṣṇunā sārdham adbhutam na śaśāka ca tam viṣṇuḥ jetum prahlādam ojasā ātmānam veda hīnam ca viṣṇuḥ prahlāda dānavāt aśaktaḥ dānavam jetum manyumān abhavat prabhuḥ prasādam tam anusmṛtya śaivam viṣṇuḥ surottamaḥ jñātvā ca balinam viṣṇum yuddha deśāt apākramat ajitam viṣṇunā ātmānam śiva tejaḥ vimohitaḥ prahrādaḥ jitam ātmānam bubudhe dānava īśvaraḥ prahrāda asura saṃgrāmam ghoram deva bhayaṃkaram abhūt abda śatam divyam viṣṇunā sārdham adbhutam na śrutena na vīryeṇa na śauryeṇa na tejasā hiraṇyakaśipuḥ na āsīt rājā tad sadṛśaḥ mahān na andhakaḥ na hiraṇyākṣaḥ prahlāda sadṛśaḥ bale bhṛgu śāpāt kila āyātaḥ daitya tvam viṣṇuḥ eva saḥ na abhavan tasya rājye tu duḥkha śoka anvitāḥ prajāḥ evam sa dānava śreṣṭhaḥ kṛtvā yuddham tu viṣṇunā paścāttāpa upatapta aṅgaḥ viṣayebhyaḥ gata spṛhaḥ vara dānam anityam tat jñātvā ca tapasaḥ phalam niḥspṛhaḥ sarva kāryeṣu viṣaya īpsā vivarjitaḥ sarva sannyasta karmā tu dhyāna abhyāsa parāyaṇaḥ punarāvartakam janma buddhvā ca anitya tām parām kaṣṭam rājyam iti jñātvā prahrādaḥ parama artha vid abhiṣicya sutam rājye ditijānām virocanam daitya dānava rājānam kṛtvā tam asura īśvaram mokṣa siddhim parām icchan pravrajyā kṛta niścayaḥ param tattvam atha anveṣṭum guṇa atītam ajam śivam sākṣāt bhagavataḥ śiṣyam kapilasya āsurim munim śiṣya tvena upasaṃgamya mokṣa vidyām avāptavān prakṛtīnām param tattvam vikārāṇām param tathā kṣetrajñam puruṣam jñātvā bhoktāram bhoktṛ varjitam mokṣa vidyā para artha jñaḥ sāṃkhya siddhānta pāragaḥ sa yogam paramam labdhvā sāṃkhya ācāryaḥ mahā muniḥ śrīmān evam tribhuvana ripuḥ daitya rājaḥ mahātmā kṛtvā vairam vibudha guruṇā viṣṇunā dīrgha kālam siddhaḥ paścāt jagat abhaya daḥ prāpta yogaḥ ca bhūtvā muktaḥ paścāt sa padam amṛtam brahma ca ādyam hi avāpa kṣetrajña boddhā guṇa viprayuktaḥ duḥkhaiḥ vihīnaḥ bhava pāśa bandhaiḥ evam tān chinna pakṣān tu girīn sarvān purandaraḥ pracakāra muni śreṣṭha prāk pṛṣṭaḥ yat aham tvayā vyāsaḥ uvāca kim anyat akarot devaḥ kim ca enam prāha pārvatī sūtaḥ uvāca ṛṣiḥ parama yogi īśaḥ kathayāmāsa tattvataḥ śreṣṭhāḥ tapasvinaḥ viprāḥ devebhyaḥ a api garīyasaḥ madīyāḥ yāḥ prajāḥ sṛṣṭāḥ prathamāḥ prabhavaḥ hi aham rudrāḥ dvijātayaḥ pūrvam mayā sṛṣṭāḥ mahā ojasaḥ madīyām sṛṣṭim ālokya brahmaṇā api sisṛkṣuṇā brāhmaṇāḥ mūrdhataḥ sṛṣṭāḥ pūrvam eva mahā prabhāḥ agra bhūtaḥ hi aham teṣām dvijaśreṣṭhaḥ tadā abhavam uttamā eṣā aparā sṛṣṭiḥ brāhmaṇāḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ dharma ādikam caturvargam sarvam teṣu pratiṣṭhitam mṛtyu janma apaham yat tat mama pāśupatam vratam na varṇāḥ sādhayanti anye muktvā brāhmaṇa sattamān lakṣaṇam sampravakṣyāmi dvijātīnām idam śṛṇu brāhmaṇaḥ tu bhavet jātyā saṃskārāt dvijaḥ ucyate vidyayā vipra tām yāti brāhmaṇyam trividham smṛtam yaḥ ca jātyā bhavet vipraḥ yaḥ ca syāt vedapāragaḥ tayoḥ trāṇe vadhe ca api puṇya pāpe same smṛte tādṛśam bhojayet śrāddhe daive karmaṇi vā api yaḥ saḥ a api śreṣṭhaḥ smṛtaḥ devi pūjitaḥ tārayet kulam sarvathā jāti mātreṇa yaḥ syāt brāhmaṇaḥ kvacid na anṛtam brāhmaṇaḥ brūte na hanti prāṇinam dvijaḥ vidvas brāhmaṇa tām labdhvā kim na labdham hi dehinā mūrtayaḥ yāḥ smṛtāḥ hi aṣṭāu arka soma ādayaḥ mama prabhavanti sadā devi viprāḥ śreṣṭhatamāḥ tatas na asādhyam vidyate teṣām dvijānām parama ojasām ruṣitāḥ te prasannāḥ vā prayacchanti śubha aśubham dvijāḥ tapasvinaḥ ye tu veda vidyā viśāradāḥ aham pitāmahaḥ ca eva teṣām yoni dvayam smṛtam mattaḥ pūrvam prasūtāḥ te tatas devi pitāmahāt āvām hetū dvijātīnām prakṛti tvāt tvam eva ca prakṛtiḥ tvam samākhyātā puruṣaḥ paramaḥ hi aham brahma viṣṇū smṛtau yau tau rajaḥ sattva guṇa ātmakau āvābhyām tau prajāyete sṛṣṭi kāle prajāpatī aham hi agniḥ tamaḥ viṣṇuḥ sattvam brahmaṇi saṃsthitam ārjavam samatām ca eva viddhi tejaḥ dvijātiṣu athā evam vartamānasya bhūyān kālaḥ jagāma ha dvitīye aatha samāyāte ṣaṣṭhe kāle sa ha dvijaḥ śīta durdina jāt doṣāt na eva atithim avaikṣata durdṛśam vṛddham ārtam ca prasravat kṛmi śoṇitam bhuṅkṣva saṃskṛtam annam tu prāpaya asmān anugraham śṛṇvan nityam idam paṭhan ca vidhivat vidvān śuciḥ mānavaḥ pārāśarya tapodhana vyāsaḥ uvāca yat jñātvā brāhmaṇāḥ siddhāḥ prāpnuyuḥ mokṣam uttamam yat mām pṛcchasi tat jñānam yogam māheśvaram param sāṃkhya yoga āgama upetāḥ sarva adhyātma viśāradāḥ yat a buddhvā mahābhāga jñānam śaṃkara bhāṣitam tat yathā kathitam teṣām tryambakeṇa divaukasām tasya aham sthita pakṣam tu saṃsāra utpatti nāśanam prakṛteḥ paramam guhyam pudgalam parameṣṭhinam yam sāṃkhyāḥ puruṣam prāhuḥ pañcaviṃśam ajam vibhum yogāḥ ca api hi ṣaḍviṃśam vyāpinam triguṇa ātmakam yaḥ aasṛjat sṛṣṭi kāle tu devau sarva jagat sṛjau prajāpatī mahātmānau dhyātvā viṣṇu pitāmahau lakṣaṇam viddhi yogasya procyamānam idam mayā chidyante paśu pāśāni yasya abhyāsāt mahā mune eṣa bhogaḥ iti vyāsa kīrtitaḥ śambhunā purā bhava janma apahaḥ sūkṣmaḥ prapannānām divaukasām śarīre tiṣṭhataḥ puṃsaḥ yadā ātmani manaḥ sthitam kṛtānām pratyayaiḥ sūkṣmaiḥ sukha duḥkha ādi saṃjñinām aśeṣa lopaḥ iti uktam sādhakasya tu yuñjataḥ abhisandhiḥ tadā yasmāt īśvarasya ātmanaḥ ca ha sukha ādānam atha ekena bhāvanā pra atha adhikaraṇam tatra kramaśas parikīrtyate pratyāhāram atas vidyā prāṇāyamaḥ tathā eva ca dhyānam ca dhāraṇā ca eva yoga abhyāsaḥ udāhṛtaḥ vaśyāḥ hi dhātavaḥ tasmāt bhavanti asya na saṃśayaḥ antarikṣe ca sarvāḥ tu kīrtitāḥ hi na saṃśayaḥ sthāna traya nibaddhāḥ tu catur lakṣaṇa saṃsthitāḥ kriyāḥ pramādinā na etāḥ śakyāḥ sādhayitum kvacid tisraḥ etāḥ sadā mṛtyum pravṛttāḥ moha lakṣaṇāḥ ajñānam kampate tāsu bahis antar carāsu api bhūta indriya manaḥ kāla diś nivṛttiḥ prabhāvataḥ daśa eva āśāḥ trayaḥ kālāḥ bhūtāni indriya vistaraḥ manaḥ pracāra saṃyuktaḥ viṣaya abhāva lakṣaṇaḥ tri kāla viṣayam jñānam aiśvarya udayam eva ca cale manasi na abhūtām bhavetām saṃsthite samam manaḥ sarvam idam yasmāt sādhanam yoga karmaṇaḥ prāṇa nigrahaṇe dhyānam prāṇāyāmam iti smṛtam prāṇaḥ eva hi vāyūnām sārathye vartate sadā prāṇa nigrahaṇe yogī nityam abhyāsam ācaret dvis apāne sakṛt prāṇe samāne ca ebhiḥ dhyāna gatiḥ sūkṣmā vāyu saṃrodha kāri tā sakṛt eva samāhārāt asakṛt parimokṣaṇāt kumbhakam ca etat uddiṣṭam prāṇāyāma ādi dhāraṇāt prāṇaḥ vāyuḥ asau ātmā śarīre jīva vidyāt rūpa dvayam tasya sthūlam sūkṣmam ca sarvatas avyaktam sūkṣma rūpam tu sthūlam ākāra lakṣaṇam sthūlāt ārabhya sūkṣmam tu dhyāna abhyāsāt nirūpayet sūkṣma saṃropaṇāt jyotiḥ prakāśaḥ jāyate mune dhyāyataḥ sthūlalakṣyam tu sarva dikṣu sṛtam manaḥ yadā dīptimat utpadyet tadā āśu sthāpayet punar girau vṛkṣe marut pṛṣṭhe śvabhre prāsāda paṅktiṣu samudre vasudhāyām vā manaḥ saṃyogam ācaret acaitanyam diśet mohāt vāyuḥ dṛṣṭam atas saṃhṛtya ca tatas vyāpi bhūyas dhyāna gatim caret pravṛtta jñāna yogasya viṣaya asaṃga saṃkṣaye prātibhāni hi vighnāni sattva ādi guṇa jāni tu ākāśagamanam divyam apsaraḥ gaṇa saṃgajam sattvāt etāni vighnāni prātibhāni tu lakṣayet rajaḥ vighnāni saṃvidyāt prātibhāni na saṃśayaḥ kṣaya śūla jvara unmāda kuṣṭha sarpa grahāṇi tu yātanā udgama pūrvāṇi tāmasāni vinirdiśet guṇatraya samutthāni prātibhāni sadā muniḥ yuñjan saṃlakṣayet śīghram cikitset āśu ca eva hi prāṇa ādyān abhisaṃdhāya manaḥ saṃhṛtya ca eva hi prāṇāyāmena kumbhena hanyāt vighnāni sarvatas nirīhaḥ nirvṛtaḥ yogī sa cetāḥ ghaṭate yadā prātibhāni tadā yānti vighnāni hi samam kṣayam prādurbhāvaḥ tatas teṣām guṇānām moha kāraṇam tamaḥ raudram vijānīyāt sattvam brahmaṇi saṃsthitam rajaḥ tu vaiṣṇavam vidyāt teṣām hantā maheśvaraḥ mahādevam param jñātvā yoga abhyāsa rataḥ dvijaḥ guṇatraya vinirmuktaḥ śaṃkare layam āpnuyāt krameṇa sa upadeśena dhyāna abhyāsam samācaret śabda ādīn viṣayān jitvā śrotra ādi indriya gocarān sa abhimānam sa niyamam tyaktvā sāṃkhya viniścayam puruṣaḥ pañcaviṃśaḥ ca sāṃkhya siddhānta gocaraḥ etat eva param guhyam sāṃkhyānām tattva viniścaye prakṛteḥ antaram jñātvā puruṣasya ca mucyate yogam sāṃkhyam ca sarvatra dvividham jñānam ucyate aṣṭau prakṛtayaḥ hi etāḥ tanmātraiḥ pañcabhiḥ saha manaḥ pañca ca bhūtāni vikārāḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ puruṣaḥ pañcaviṃśaḥ ca sarva ārambha vivarjitaḥ nityaḥ sarva gataḥ sūkṣmaḥ cetāvān triguṇa ujjhitaḥ saṃśuddhaḥ vimalaḥ bhoktā akartā sākṣi vat sthitaḥ viditvā na viśanti enam bhūyas saṃsāra pañjaram pañcaviṃśati tattvāni sāṃkhyaiḥ etāni yogibhiḥ sūkṣmā prakṛtiḥ avyaktā bhojyā sarva ratā calā nityā ca acetanā ca eva vijñeyā sarvakāmikī bhinna kārya pracārāṇām eteṣām guṇa saṃjñinām hetunā puruṣa arthena sādhikā vṛttiḥ ucyate avikalpyam ca mānuṣyam sarvam etat guṇa ātmakam mūḍham niścetanam bhūtam sthūlam sūkṣmam atha api vā mokṣa vighna karāḥ hi ete kīrtitāḥ yoginaḥ guṇāḥ vibhāgena vikāra sthāḥ yathādharmam samāśritāḥ bhūta sargaḥ tridhā bhūtaḥ sāṃkhya jñaiḥ paripaṭhyate saṃsiddhaḥ prākṛtaḥ ca eva tathā vaikṛtaḥ ucyate tatra dharmeṇa saṃsiddhāḥ yoge māheśvare sthitāḥ na icchanti saṃbhavam vyāsa pañca ātmataḥ smṛtaḥ te punar dravya saṃbandhāt sāṃkhya dṛṣṭānta paṇḍitāḥ icchanti kāryam āśritya kāraṇa āśrayiṇaḥ na hi pradhānāt puruṣāt buddhiḥ abhūt sattva adhikā asakṛt guṇānām pariṇāmena prākṛtānām jala ādi vat vyavasāyaḥ tathā rūpam niścayaḥ ca iti bhāṣyate aham eva hi kartā iti ahaṃkāraḥ udāhṛtaḥ buddhi bhūtāt ahaṃkārāt sattva utpannāt prakāśinaḥ buddhīndriyāṇi jātāni pañca sattva udayāni tu atas karmendriyāṇi āhuḥ guṇāt jātāni rājasāt tāmasāt api ahaṃkārāt tanmātrāṇi abhavan punar śaktam sarva āśrayam sūkṣmam anivārita dikpatham liṅgam tat ca samākhyātam dehinām yat ca kāraṇam prākṛtasya vikārasya dehabhedāḥ sahasraśas tattva sargaḥ smṛtaḥ pūrvaḥ piṇḍa sargaḥ tatas pratyayānām tatas sargaḥ tṛtīyaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ bhedāḥ pratyaya sarge tu vaiṣamyāt apare smṛtāḥ pañcāśat paritas bhedāḥ ye ca teṣām guṇāḥ daśa ṣaṣṭiḥ ete padārthāḥ hi gīyante kapila ādibhiḥ sā tuṣṭiḥ navadhā bhinnā siddhiḥ ca eva aṣṭadhā punar smṛtaḥ pañcavidhaḥ ca api teṣām eva viparyayaḥ aṣṭa bhedāḥ tri bhinnā syāt daśabhiḥ kārya bandhanaiḥ ete ṣaṣṭi padārthāḥ tu saṃkhyā jñānam iti śrutiḥ nyūnām eka adhikām eke ṣaṣṭim anye tu jānate sāṃkhya tantram mahat buddhvā jñātvā tat prakṛteḥ param pañcaviṃśam ajam tattvam bhoktāram abhimāninam saṃdeha apagamaḥ na abhūt a buddhvā parameśvaram sva yogam śambhunā vatsa grāhitaḥ gata saṃśayaḥ tadā ṣaḍviṃśakam tattvam jñātvā sarvagam īśvaram yaḥ tayoḥ paratas nityaḥ ṣaḍviṃśaḥ tu īśvaraḥ vibhuḥ tam jñātvā paramam tattvam mucyate janma bandhanāt sāṃkhyam kṛtsnam api jñātvā guṇa antara vicāraṇāt pañcaviṃśaka saṃbodhāt nā eva muktiḥ prajāyate sa baddhaḥ bhoktṛ pāśaiḥ tu guṇaiḥ prakṛti saṃbhavaiḥ abhimāna ātmakaḥ bhoktā puruṣa tvāt amocakaḥ eṣaḥ uktaḥ samāsena sāṃkhya siddhānta vistaraḥ yoga abhyāsam para asaṃgam sūkṣma jñānam aninditam matam paśupateḥ kṛtsnam amṛta āspada lambhanam ākāśaḥ pavanaḥ tejaḥ bhūmiḥ āpaḥ tathā eva ca eṣām ekatra saṃghātāt śarīram nāma jāyate rasāḥ soma ātmakāḥ sarve tathā āhāraḥ caturvidhaḥ dehinām tu rasa utpattiḥ āhārasya niṣevaṇāt rasāt rakta samutpattiḥ śoṇitāt piśitam smṛtam medaḥ utpadyate māṃsāt tasmāt asthīni ca abhavan majjā ca asthi bhavā jñeyā tatas śukra samudbhavaḥ rakta śukra samāyogāt garbhaḥ saṃbhavati striyāḥ ṛtu yoga samādhānāt daiva karma vaśāt api piśitam sapta rātreṇa māsāt ca aṅkura saṃbhavaḥ śiraḥ pāṇi mukha skandha grīvā pṛṣṭha ādi saṃyutaḥ pañcānām atha māsānām akṣi karṇa ādi saṃbhavaḥ pañcabhūta ātmakam tat tu cetaḥ vat avatiṣṭhati idānīm jāta mātraḥ tu yogam eva abhyāsāmi aham na punar yena saṃsāram pravekṣyāmi aśubha udayam deham āviśate vāyuḥ smṛtim ākṣipya tāmasaḥ āgama anveṣaṇe saktāḥ pāpa anuṣṭhāna varjitāḥ matvā saṃsāra jam kleśam janma mṛtyu bhaya arditam vairāgyam janmataḥ kṛtvā sarva ārambha parikṣayāt sarveṣām yoginām guhyam sarva yoga nidarśanam tatas māheśvaram yogam ākhyāsye tat param tava teja agniḥ kāma jaḥ vahniḥ krodha agniḥ vīkṣaṇa analaḥ jaṭhara agniḥ ca bhūtātmā pañca ete deha vahnayaḥ pañca ete vāyavaḥ nityāḥ dehinaḥ deha saṃbhavāḥ ākāśa prabhavāḥ hi ete śarīram ca eṣu tiṣṭhati māṃsa rakta samākīrṇam snāyu medaḥ sthi saṃcayam tvacā avanaddham durgandham śakṛt mūtra samāvṛtam taḍit abhra āgama anityam mṛd pātra āpāta saṃnibham sarva anartha samāyuktam na cirāt nāśa gāmi ca eka rūpam nava dvāram tri sthūṇam pañca devatam viṣaya indriya saṃkīrṇam na eka vyādhi sama arditam prāṇa ādibhiḥ avaṣṭabdham śarīram yantra vat sthitam āma pātram iva āpātāt śīryate kāla paryayāt pañcabhūtāni bhūtāni dehāt niṣkramya sarvaśas āviśanti tathā ātmānam ātmā ca viśate drutam bhūteṣu pavanaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ jīvaḥ tad vinibandhanaḥ vāyu agnī sahitau jīvaḥ na jīvaḥ prāṇinām pṛthak mūrtiḥ anyā na jīvasya mṛtyau dṛśyati dehinaḥ pañca rūpāt paraḥ devaḥ sūkṣmāt sūkṣmataraḥ mataḥ na jānāti na ca sparśet jīvaḥ prāṇa samāvṛtaḥ prāṇaḥ nāma mahā vāyuḥ jīvam dhārayate dvija sa prāṇaḥ dhārayati agnim sa ca jīvaḥ iti smṛtaḥ vāyu saṃdhāraṇaḥ hi agniḥ kriyāsu parivartate evam etat śarīram tu pañca bhūta guṇa ātmakam hṛdaye karṇikāyām tu madhya sthaḥ tejasām nidhiḥ ātmā sarva gataḥ nityam varjitaḥ viṣaya ādibhiḥ bhābhiḥ prakāśitaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ vikāraiḥ vipramocitaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ bhavati vipra indra muktiḥ ca eva tatas smṛtā ṣaḍviṃśaka parijñānāt layam ādhāya tatra hi sarva prāṇi gatam sūkṣmam ātma dehe ca saṃsthitam yaḥ a abhyupāyaḥ paraḥ tasya vijñānāt īśvarasya tu yoga abhyāsam param tam te pravakṣyāmi dvijottama antarātmā ca cetovān manaḥ saṃkalpa jam smṛtam mahābhūta pracārāt ca kṣobha agniḥ jīvaḥ ucyate na śarīram vinā cetaḥ tat ced nā asti kutas ca tat cetaḥ na vinā jīvam sa ca api na vinā manaḥ yasya cetaḥ teṣām sa cetovān prakīrtitaḥ cetasā tu vijānāti śubha aśubha nibandhanam śabda ādi viṣaya atītaḥ indriyāṇi nigṛhya tu jīvaḥ bandha ātmakaḥ jñeyaḥ śubha aśubha nibandhanāt udrekāt jāyate duḥkham saukhyam sattvāt prajāyate saukhyasya pariṇāmāt hi duḥkha utpattiḥ śarīriṇām sukha duḥkha ātmakam moham bhūtāni āviśya tiṣṭhati manaḥ cetaḥ samādhānāt viṣayaḥ udvijanāt api tasya nāśaḥ samākhyātaḥ saṃgasya ca vivarjanāt icchā nigrahaṇe saktāḥ krodha ādi parivarjitāḥ ātmā sarva gataḥ hi eṣaḥ sūkṣmaḥ saṃbhoga varjitaḥ atha aiśvaryam param dhyānāt yoga jam prāpnute muniḥ asukhāni api karmāṇi mahā pāpa kṛtāni api prāpya karma kṣayam kṛtsnam dhyāna yuktyā mahā mune kāma roṣau tathā saṃgaḥ dveṣaḥ rāga viniścayaḥ pāruṣyam krūra tā ca eva bhāvāḥ kṣatra nibandhanāḥ moha bhāva antara atītaḥ sama duḥkha sukhaḥ sadā dhyānena kṣīyate karma pāpam vā yadi vā śubham kṣīṇa karmā tatas yogī tat param brahma vindati yoginam puruṣam dṛṣṭvā pāpaiḥ mucyanti tad kṣaṇāt yogam eva param jñātvā sarva aśubha vimocanam dhyānam dhyeyam param ca eva dhātāram ca eva ye viduḥ tāpanāt tapanāt tāpyāt muktāḥ te duḥkha bandhanāt nirguṇaḥ sāṃkhya mukhyānām puruṣaḥ paripaṭhyate dīpa nirvāṇa vat teṣām dehabhede gatiḥ smṛtā īśvaraḥ sarva nirmuktaḥ kṣetrajñaḥ yoginām mataḥ bhikṣā upātta rasa āhāraḥ mumukṣuḥ iti śabdyate karma mārgāt atikrāntaḥ dhyānī iti parikīrtyate na hṛṣyati sukhe ca eva dhyānī iti sa viśabditaḥ pūrvaḥ saṃketanaḥ proktaḥ paścāt saṃkathanaḥ smṛtaḥ saṃsaktaḥ ca tṛtīyaḥ tu teṣām bhedam imam śṛṇu pratirūpam vibhoḥ kṛtvā daśabhiḥ niyamaiḥ yutaḥ veda ādṛta sukha āsaṃgaḥ sattva karmā tu abāndhavān sarva ārambha parityāgāt kapila ādi niṣevitaḥ smṛtaḥ saṃkathanaḥ panthāḥ yoga agni paridīpakaḥ samyak etān muniḥ jñātvā nirmamaḥ śritaḥ sarvān dhyāna vidhān tu etān ucyamānān imān mayā śṛṇu vyāsa mahābuddhe yoga aiśvarya phala pradān pañcaviṃśatimam tattvam kṣaram tat prakṛteḥ param akṣaram ca param brahma ṣaḍviṃśakam iti smṛtam manaḥ eka gatam kṛtvā dhāraṇābhiḥ punar punar vāyu nigrahaṇam kuryāt pratyāhāra puraḥsaram muhūrta ardham kṣaṇam vā api pratyāhāram samācaret talāt triṃśat śatam ca ūrdhvam prāṇāyāma nibandhanam tat baddhvā vijane deśe dhyāna abhyāsam samācaret kṣudhā ārtaḥ vā atibhuktaḥ vā rujā duḥkha anvitaḥ vā a spṛśan daśanaiḥ dantān oṃkāram brahma saṃsmaran dhyāyīta nirmamaḥ vidvān prāṇāyāma puraḥsaram nirīhaḥ pihita akṣaḥ ca nikṣipya hṛdi dhāraṇām pāṣāṇaḥ iva niṣpandaḥ muniḥ dhyānam samācaret ekatra aspanda saṃdhānam manasaḥ dhāraṇā smṛtā vikārāḥ guṇa jāḥ sarve naśyante vighna hetavaḥ anveṣṭi ca param brahma sva śarīram samāsthitam kṛtvā aikyam prāṇa manasām indriyāṇām vinigrahāt sarva bhāva parityāgāt mokṣam āpnoti asaṃśayam atha aiśvarya gatām siddhim dhyāyan aṣṭavidhām yadā paśyate yoga saṃsiddhyā tadā yogī prakīrtyate brahma soma ādikān devān yadā yuktaḥ prapaśyati manasā yoginām siddhiḥ sāṃkhyāḥ tattva viniścayāt na viśeṣaḥ yogānām muktvā māheśvaram vidhim yoginaḥ dhyāna saṃsiddhim sāṃkhyāḥ tattva viniścayam śabda ādīn viṣayān yuktaḥ na dhyāyan vetti vai yadā araṇye maunam ādhāya dhyāyet oṃkāram akṣaram tri saṃsthānam tri mātram ca tri brahma tri akṣaram tathā mātrā hīnam sa mātram ca yaḥ vidyāt sa vimucyate oṃkāraḥ paramam tattvam ātmā brahma ca kīrtyate kṣetrajñaiḥ ātma saṃbodhāt yogibhiḥ sūkṣma darśanāt īśvaram prāpnute yogī yam avāpya vimucyate indriyāṇi tu sarvāṇi śabda rūpa ādi nigrahāt viditvā karma jān hanti yogī doṣān aśeṣatas dhyāna śaktyā tatas śuddhaḥ sarvajit mokṣam āpnute yuñjan pratidinam vyāsa ṣaṣ māsāt yogam āpnute tam ātmānam param jñātvā yoga śaktyā mahā muniḥ parama aiśvarya saṃyuktaḥ amṛtam padam āpnuyāt pañcabhūtam manaḥ ca atra vikārāḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ mahān buddhiḥ ahaṃkāraḥ tanmātrāṇi ca pañca hi imāḥ prakṛtayaḥ vyāsa deham aṣṭau samāśritāḥ nābhi madhye tu nāḍīnām śatam ekam pratiṣṭhitam sarva aṅgeṣu tu yāḥ tu anyāḥ jala anna rasa vāhikāḥ tāsām triṃśat sahasrāṇi vyāna srotasi saṃsthitāḥ udānasya aparāḥ nāḍyaḥ brahma mūrdhni pratiṣṭhitāḥ cakranābhī samāśritya bhūta saṃghātam āsthitam tad madhye tu guṇa spṛṣṭaḥ karma ātmā bhūta mohanaḥ vighāta jananaḥ sūrya ayuta nibhaḥ sthitaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ a agniḥ iva utpātī giri nīhāra saṃnibhaḥ guṇa sthaḥ bhūtayoniḥ tu puruṣaḥ pañcaviṃśakaḥ nirdhūma aṅgāra saṃkāśaḥ na vijñāta āgama kriyaḥ ṣaḍviṃśaḥ puruṣaḥ sūkṣmaḥ īśvaraḥ paramaḥ vibhuḥ saḥ eṣa kathitaḥ vyāsa pareṣām paratas sthitaḥ mahādevam imam jñātvā mucyate tad kṣaṇāt muniḥ guṇatraya vikāra sthaḥ kathitaḥ pañcaviṃśakaḥ bhūyas saṃsarate tasmāt ajñānena eva mohitaḥ deva mānuṣa tiryakṣu bhrāmyate rāga mohitaḥ ṣaḍviṃśaka samādhānāt siddhiḥ tasya vikārataḥ kriyā kārya vinirmuktaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvasṛj prabhuḥ parama aiśvarya saṃpūrṇaḥ kṛtakṛtyaḥ maheśvaraḥ ajaḥ svayambhūḥ ṣaḍviṃśaḥ kūṭasthaḥ hi acalaḥ dhruvaḥ tatas tattvāni jāyante puruṣaḥ ca guṇaiḥ saha tasmin eva ca līyante ṣaḍviṃśe tu maheśvare yathā agniḥ nirdahet ante bhūta grāmam aśeṣatas ṣaḍviṃśaka padārtha jñaḥ tathā karmāṇi nirdahet sarva vyāpi yathā ākāśam sūkṣmam na eva tu lipyate ṣaḍviṃśaka rataḥ yogī tathā pāpaiḥ na lipyate ṣaḍviṃśāt paratas na asti tasmāt tad paramaḥ bhavet brahma viṣṇu ādikam sarvam bhūta grāmam aśeṣatas īśvarāt jāyate vyāsa tasmin eva pralīyate saḥ eṣa sarvagaḥ devaḥ ṣaḍviṃśaḥ parameśvaraḥ ye pañca vāyavaḥ dehe teṣām nigrahaṇe yatet apānam ca samāṇam ca kṛtvā eka gatam ādṛtaḥ vyānena sahitam prāṇam udāne saṃniveśayet nāsā agre tu sakṛt prāṇam prāpayet yoga śaktitaḥ bhadrām dṛṣṭim samāsthāya ṣaḍviṃśam cintayet tatas mātrāḥ pañcadaśa proktāḥ prathamam sandhibandhanam mātrāḥ viṃśati vijñeyāḥ pañcamaḥ sandhi vigrahaḥ ekam āsanam āsthāya ṣaḍviṃśam samanusmaran sarva aṅgāni samāhṛtya niṣpandaḥ dhyānam ācaret śanais saṃcārayet vāyum nāḍibhiḥ srotasi sthitam tāvat tiṣṭheta yukta ātmā dhyāna yogāt gata spṛhaḥ soma maṇḍala saṃkāśam yāvat paśyet hṛdi sthitam jyotiḥ deha āśritam dṛṣṭvā yoga aiśvaryam pravartate tri kāla viṣayam jñānam pratyakṣam yoginaḥ bhavet sarva vidyā vidām vyāsa yatīnām vrata cāriṇām tapasvinām ca viprāṇām yogī śreṣṭhaḥ udāhṛtaḥ na anyathā mucyate jantuḥ saṃsārāt tamasaḥ parāt ṣaḍviṃśaka parijñānam muktvā yoga nibandhanam jitvā hi aṣṭa prakāram tu āsanānām nibandhanam mātrā utkarṣeṇa jitvā ca prāṇāyāmam caturvidham tanmātrāṇi indriyaiḥ jitvā viṣayaiḥ indriyāṇi ca yoga āspadam samādhāya pūrva uktam guṇa varjitam dhyāyīta hṛdi tam devam viśvam viśveśvaram vibhum an ādi nidhanam śāntam traiguṇya parivarjitam tālau jihvām samādhāya dantaiḥ dantān na ca spṛśan kṛtvā manasi tat sarvam prāṇa apānau niruddhya ca oṃkāram tu asakṛt dhyāyan saṃhṛtya aṅgāni kūrma vat śarīram sarvam āpūrya vikṣipet sarvam ātmani tat saṃhṛtya muniḥ bhūyas vyāpi bhūtam pravistṛtam nāḍī vāyu pracāreṇa bhūyas bhūyas tathā ārabhet madhya aṅge ca aparāhaṇe ca kṛṣṇam dhyāyīta tāmasam taijasam sarvatas ca api dhyānadevam jayet guṇān yena ṣaḍviṃśakam buddhvā hṛdaya stham maheśvaram paramātmā param brahma kāraṇam puruṣaḥ mahān īśvaraḥ nirguṇaḥ bhoktā ṣaḍviṃśaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ jāgarti svapate bhuṅkte saṃtiṣṭhati dadāti ca sa sraṣṭā sa ca saṃhartā guṇa atītaḥ maheśvaraḥ yogam pāśupatam tubhyam ādyam mokṣa gati pradam sūkṣmam tat paramam jñānam pravakṣyāmi tava anagha yasya samyak parijñānāt vijñeyam na aparam bhavet yadā bhasma praviṣṭāḥ te tejaḥ śāṃkaram uttamam yaḥ yogaḥ sakalaḥ vyāsa kṛcchra upāya prasādhanaḥ yaḥ a ayam bhagavatā sākṣāt yogaḥ māheśvaraḥ paraḥ tattvataḥ kalpitaḥ teṣām prapannānām divaukasām vratam pāśupatam prāpya ṣaṣ māsāt jñānam āpnute sarva dharmam bahis kṛtvā niṣakalasya prabhāvataḥ sarva jñāna samāyuktam tapaḥ dharma bahiṣkṛtam pāpiṣṭhāḥ dharmavantaḥ vā bhaktāḥ kevalam īśvaram ātmānam yaḥ paśum kṛtvā mahādevam hutāśanam dīkṣitaḥ brahma bhasmabhyām śiva yājī muniḥ bhavet payasi iva ghṛtam vyāsa yam viditvā vimucyate yathā tat akṣaram brahma procyamānam tathā śṛṇu prakṛti strī tvam āsthāya nityam tau īśa pārśva gau tu prakṛtiḥ khyātā akṣaraḥ tu svayam īśvaraḥ īśvarāt nirgatā sā hi prakṛtiḥ guṇa bandhanā tasyāḥ sraṣṭā mahādevaḥ yatas viṣṇu pitāmahau yena eva dhyāninā vyāsaḥ īśvaraḥ vikalpitaḥ mahā mohau tamaḥ niṣṭhau viṣaya asaṅga saṃyutau vibhaktāḥ kramaśas dehe vibhāgena yathāśrayam hṛdaye karṇikāyām tu yat padmam sarvatomukham veṣṭitam tantubhiḥ sūkṣmaiḥ tat ūrdhvam sarva dehinām śayānam pratibuddham vā yāḥ tāḥ bibhrati dehinam sthitaḥ manasi sūkṣmaḥ tu śītāṃśuḥ nāma tantukaḥ manaḥ somaḥ sukham brahmā jñānam dharmaḥ ratiḥ tathā kāma roṣau jarā tṛṣṇā adharmaḥ ca iti tāmasāḥ madhye tasyāḥ sthitam saumyam amṛtam śaśi maṇḍalam yā amṛtā iti aparā nāḍī sā tu svapne pratiṣṭhitā ubhau etau muniḥ jitvā svapna jāgaraṇa āśrayau vahneḥ yat auṣṇyam tat pāpam tejaḥ dharmaḥ udāhṛtaḥ prakṛtiḥ yā sa jīvaḥ tu yā śikhā sa maheśvaraḥ saḥ evam dṛśyate dhyānāt na anya upāyāt kathaṃcana sa jīvaḥ kīrtitaḥ vyāsa sthiti hetuḥ śarīriṇām mahānāgaḥ smṛtaḥ nāmnā vāyuḥ ākāśa gocaraḥ ākāśe karṇikāyām ca nāḍyām jīve ca saṃsthitaḥ gāndhārī vijayā ca eva saṃjayā tala vartinī nāgaḥ etāsu nāḍīṣu vāyuḥ samparivartate sā jayā vijayā yā ca dve te prāṇa vahe smṛte yogāt apāna manasoḥ nāgena samudīritāḥ vṛddham saṃdhārayet deham āyuṣā ca balena ca sā asmin sa dhātuke dehe kūrmāre ca anile sthitā gulphe kara tale saktā nikuñcā sa prasāriṇī adhas sthaḥ pāda sambaddhaḥ ūrdhva gaḥ śirasi sthitaḥ prāṇāḥ te pañca vikhyātāḥ vahnayaḥ yājinaḥ netreṣu dṛśyate yaḥ aayam jyotiḥ chāyā prakāśakaḥ vyāsaḥ uvāca īśvara ādhāra samprāpteḥ upāyam mokṣa lambhanam sva deham ripu vat tyaktvā ṣaḍviṃśe layam āpnuyāt tam āśritya āsthitāḥ nāḍyaḥ nābhyām pādeṣu adhas gatāḥ śiraḥ baddhāḥ smṛtāḥ bhūyas tāḥ eva karam āsthitāḥ karam āśritya tāḥ bhūyas karṇikām punar āśritāḥ yā prabhā durdṛśā tasya tām bhajet yoga vid muniḥ vāyuḥ dhanaṃjayaḥ nāmnā yaḥ hṛdi sphurate sadā tāluke hṛdi vā baddham trayam nābhyām pratiṣṭhitam yathā jīvaḥ na yoginaḥ tat idam śṛṇu bāhyaḥ śarīra jaḥ ca eva mṛtyuḥ dvividhaḥ ucyate viṣa śastra agni yogaiḥ tu sarpa daṃṣṭri marut jalaiḥ evam agnau praṇaṣṭe tu sarve eva anilāḥ tatas niṣkramanti tatas dehāt tvaritam vāyunā īritāḥ prāk upāttaḥ tatas dehaḥ sūkṣmaḥ vijāyate sa tatra sandhitaḥ jīvaḥ bhūyas mūḍhaḥ ca tiṣṭhati vinā yogam na jīyeta sūkṣmam māheśvaram padam upaviṣṭaḥ prasuptaḥ vā utkrānti abhyasta saṃyamaḥ sva icchayā mucyate vyāsa jñātvā tam tu maheśvaram vīkṣate ca īśvaram brahma ṣaḍviṃśam sva tanu sthitam sukham ārohati brahma harmya pṛṣṭham talāt iva evam pāśupatāḥ viprāḥ niṣkalam tam maheśvaram bālakhilya purogāḥ ca siddhāḥ ca kapila ādayaḥ paramāṇuḥ asau īśaḥ bhāva gamyaḥ manīṣiṇām sraṣṭṛ tvam jñāna vairāgyāt ātma bodhaḥ maheśa tā aṣṭamam dhruvam adhyeyam akṣaram mukti kāraṇam māyā dhāma tad īśasya jagat saṃhāra hetumat ūrdhvatas brahma lokasya yojanānām mahātmanaḥ viṃśatīnām tu koṭīnām puram sthāṇoḥ samāsthitam asaṃkhyātāḥ pure tasmin nityāḥ sūkṣmāḥ tathā avyayāḥ jñāna adhikāra dharmiṇyaḥ śaivī prakṛtayaḥ sthitāḥ yābhyaḥ jajñe mahā īśānau brahma viṣṇū prajāpatī tābhyām prakṛti bhūtābhyām sraṣṭṛ tvam paramam dadau akṣayaḥ vimalaḥ sūkṣmaḥ niṣkalaḥ sakalaḥ ca yadā āsīt haimam aṇḍam tu laukikam tu āpavargikam sura asura ādi sṛṣṭi artham brahma tvam punar āsthitā jarā mṛtyu śrama vyādhi kopa moha mada ādayaḥ tat puram vṛṣabhāṅkasya sarva vyāpi samṛddhimat meru śṛṅga pratīkāśaḥ diś bhadraḥ tapana prabhaḥ śuśubhe tat puram kīrṇam pādapaiḥ giri sannibhaiḥ śiṣyaḥ siddhaḥ pañcaśikhaḥ tathā kedāraḥ madhyamaḥ ca eva girīśaḥ ca mahālaye avimukteśvaraḥ ca eva yāni ca anyāni bhū tale eṣa te kathitaḥ sarvaḥ yogaḥ māheśvaraḥ paraḥ yajña dharma pracāra artham vedam svāyambhuva smṛtam upasadya muni indram tam bhasma saṃskāram āptavān vāyuḥ uvāca abhyasya śāṃkaram yogam īśvare layam āpnuta sāyujyam śṛṇuyāt vibhoḥ ādyam puṇyam umāpateḥ dvija vṛṣāḥ stotra anuvādam param sarve te jvalat arka vahni vapuṣaḥ tri akṣāḥ śaśāṅka tviṣaḥ śubham mārgaḥ aryamā bahubhiḥ bāṇaiḥ prahlādam daitya puṅgavam ājaghāna ratham ghoram dhvajam ca asya vyaśātayat jaghāna bahubhiḥ bāṇaiḥ vṛṣṭyā meghaḥ yathā acalam śarān a gaṇayan eva hiraṇyakaśipoḥ sutaḥ nirjitāḥ tarjitāḥ ca eva yuddhāya ca punar sthitāḥ paraspara jigīṣūṇām yuddham bhīru bhaya āvaham tatas bheryaḥ ca śaṅkhāḥ ca paṭaha āsekya puṣkalāḥ jharjharyaḥ pepukāḥ ca eva ḍiḍṇimāḥ goviṣāṇakāḥ avādyanta bale vyāse ubhayeṣām sahasraśas keśa śaivāla bahulā dhanuḥ jyā kṛta tantukā niṣṭanat śastra maṇḍūkā dukūla chatra phenilā uṣṇīṣa vara haṃsā ca kara pāda śira upalā hāra keyūra sikatā mukuṭa utphulla paṅkajā medaḥ usra kapha paṅkā ca prāṇa jīvita nāśinī tasyām snāyanti rakṣāṃsi pibanti ca ramanti ca nṛtyanti ca api bhaya dāḥ tathā gṛdhrāḥ gomāyavaḥ ca eva śvānaḥ mārjāra vāyasāḥ cakṣūṃṣi kara pāda antram bhakṣayantaḥ mama ayam hastinaḥ pādaḥ mama ayam meda paṭṭakaḥ mama ayam ca karaḥ śukraḥ bāhuḥ ca rudhirasya daḥ mama ayam tava na ayam ca gṛhṇa paśya iti ca eva hi kalaham cakrire tatra krūrāḥ piśita bhojanāḥ tyaktvā anye ca hatān tatra sva dehān su mahā rathāḥ vimānaiḥ strī jana ākīrṇaiḥ prayayuḥ brahmaṇaḥ sadaḥ apare pāpa karmāṇaḥ hanyamānāḥ ruṣā andhitaiḥ kecid ārtta svaram kṛtvā ṣa arditāḥ mriyante puruṣāḥ yuddhe śataśas aatha sahasraśas daśantaḥ ca tṛṇāni anye bhrātar putra iti ca apare krandamānāḥ janāḥ bhītāḥ prāṇān kṛcchreṇa tatyajuḥ śara sannata marmāṇaḥ patanti urvyām drumāḥ iva aśivāni śivāḥ neduḥ aṭṭahāsān ca rākṣasāḥ ārtta nādān ca puruṣāḥ tathā eva gaja vājinaḥ siṃhanādāḥ ca śūrāṇām bāhu śabdāḥ ca puṣkalāḥ dhanuṣām kṛṣyamāṇānām śabdāḥ vādya ravāḥ tathā varuṇaḥ marutaḥ ca eva viśvedevāḥ ca sarvaśas mitraḥ ca eva jayantaḥ ca bhagaḥ pūṣā aryamā tathā kāpālī ca api yaḥ rudraḥ vasavaḥ ca mahā balāḥ ete ca anye ca devānām pravarāḥ bāhu śālinaḥ prabhañjanāḥ iva akāle mahāntaḥ iva ca acalāḥ grasantaḥ ca yathā grāhāḥ chindantaḥ makarāḥ iva varṣamāṇāḥ iva ghanāḥ siṃḍāḥ iva ca nardinaḥ dānavānām balam sarve vegena eva abhyavārayan
